# Harry Potter and the Philosopher’s Stone — Panel Layout

Reference storyboard for the first film, told in original wording. It is a reading layout for imagining the story, not a transcript. Speech is paraphrased in Hogwarts English: teachers are formal, pupils sound like a British boarding school, and Hagrid is broad and warm. Names, houses, spells, and magical objects keep their proper terms. Professor Snape addresses Harry as Mr Potter, never by his first name.

How people address each other, and which funny, embarrassing, and disgusting beats must be kept, is in [address-and-famous-beats.md](address-and-famous-beats.md). Later dialogue should follow that note. This storyboard is not rewritten there.

Drawing rules for later pictures are in [rules.md](rules.md): one costume per scene, the same creature and prop every time, all four houses when the whole school is in the frame, a visible link when someone casts a spell, and a moving staircase as one rigid flight hinged at the bottom. The locks are in [wardrobes.md](wardrobes.md), [characters.md](characters.md), and [props.md](props.md). Those notes do not replace this storyboard.

- **21 chapters**. Where a comic chapter matches a novel chapter, it uses the novel name. First Lessons is our name, because that chapter was split out of the classes.
- **4 pages** in every chapter.
- **6 panels** on every page.
- **504 panels** in all.
- A code such as `C1-P2-P6` names that picture and the file `art/c1-p2-p6.jpg`. Codes stay with the picture. Inserting a chapter does not rename them, so later chapters still contain `C8-` and `C12-` codes. A panel marked not drawn has no file and must not reuse a jpg.

Read each page left to right, top to bottom, in a grid of three columns and two rows. Panel 6 is always the bottom-right page-turn. The last panel of page 4 closes the chapter.

Each panel has:

- **Beat** — the story point.
- **Image** — the picture itself: framing, light, and what the eye hits first.
- **Setting** — place, time, and background.
- **Who** — everyone in the frame, what they wear, and what they hold.
- **Faces** — expression, and who or what they look at.
- **Dialogue** — who says which line, in that paraphrased Hogwarts voice, or no dialogue.

## People and clothes

These looks stay the same unless a panel says otherwise. If a panel is brief, use the lock in [wardrobes.md](wardrobes.md). Harry’s scar stays on his right brow. The winter jumpers are locked there too. House colors start at the Sorting, not on the train. On the Hogwarts Express, first years wear travel clothes.

- **Baby Harry.** A small infant with a tuft of jet-black hair, wrapped in a soft green blanket. A fresh lightning-shaped cut on his forehead.
- **Harry, age 10–11.** Small for his age, messy black hair that will not lie flat, round wire glasses often taped at the bridge, a lightning scar on his right brow. Before Diagon Alley, and on the Hogwarts Express before the Sorting, he wears Dudley’s faded castoffs: a gray T-shirt too wide at the neck, jeans too long and rolled, scuffed sneakers, a thin jacket that does not fit. No school robe, no house tie, no scarf, no crest on the train. In the alley, after Madam Malkin, he tries on black robes over his shirt and jeans. From the Sorting onward: black Hogwarts robes over a white shirt, gray V-neck sweater, red-and-gold striped tie, black trousers, black shoes. In the castle he often adds a red-and-gold scarf. At night: a plain gray pajama set, too big.
- **Dumbledore.** Very tall and thin, long silver hair and beard, half-moon spectacles. Deep purple robes patterned with silver stars, a tall pointed hat, plum cloak. He carries a silver cigarette-lighter-shaped device, the Deluminator, that pulls light out of lamps and can send that light back. It is not his wand.
- **McGonagall.** Sharp face, dark hair in a tight bun, square spectacles, emerald-green robes and a pointed hat. As a cat: a thin tabby with spectacle-like markings around the eyes.
- **Hagrid.** Half-giant, huge shoulders, wild black hair and a tangled beard, beetle-black eyes, kind face. A long brown moleskin overcoat with many pockets, a pink umbrella strapped inside, enormous boots. He often carries a lantern.
- **Vernon Dursley.** Big, short neck, black mustache. Brown business suit, or later a straining knit vest in the shack. His face goes purple when he is angry.
- **Petunia Dursley.** Thin, long neck, pale, blonde hair pinned tight. A mint housedress and apron at home; a belted coat in the storm.
- **Dudley Dursley.** Blond, round, about Harry’s age and much heavier. Striped polo, tight shorts, new sneakers. At the zoo, a bright jacket.
- **Ron Weasley.** Tall and thin, freckles, long nose, bright red hair. On the Express: a worn maroon hand-knit with a gold R, a hand-me-down jacket and trousers, dirt on his nose, scuffed shoes. After the Sorting: secondhand black robes a little short at the wrist, the maroon sweater underneath. He carries a rat, Scabbers, and sometimes a bent wand.
- **Hermione Granger.** Bushy brown hair, large front teeth. On the Express: her own Muggle traveling clothes, a white blouse, a plain brown cardigan, a gray skirt, books hugged to her chest. After the Sorting: neat and new black robes, red-and-gold tie perfectly knotted. She often holds a wand correctly, unlike the boys.
- **Draco Malfoy.** Pale, pointed face, slicked platinum-blond hair. On the Express: an expensive plain black coat, no green lining, no snake, no house tie. Crabbe and Goyle are larger, silent, in bulky dark coats with no green. After the Sorting: expensive black robes with a green lining, and the same two boys in bulky black robes.
- **Snape.** Sallow, hooked nose, greasy shoulder-length black hair, black eyes. Buttoned black robes that billow. He rarely blinks when he looks at Harry.
- **Quirrell.** Pale, timid, a large purple turban, layered purple robes. He stammers with his shoulders until the last chamber, where he stands straight.
- **Neville Longbottom.** Round face, sandy hair. On the Express: rumpled traveling clothes. After the Sorting: robes slightly crooked, a toad named Trevor in a pocket or in his hands.
- **Madam Hooch.** Yellow hawk-like eyes, short gray hair, a whistle, and a dark flying cloak.
- **Oliver Wood.** Older student, brown hair, Gryffindor Quidditch robes, a leather guard on one arm, intense eyes.
- **Ollivander.** Pale silvery eyes, wispy silver hair, a dark old-fashioned suit. He appears very close, very suddenly.
- **Firenze.** A palomino centaur: human torso, horse body, long blond hair, calm stern face, a bow on his back. No shirt. The coat of the horse half is cream-gold.

Muggle places look like early-1990s Britain. Wizard places are stone, candlelight, and moving wood. Night scenes are lit by lamps, fire, or wands, never by a flat modern glare.

---

## Chapter 1 — The Boy Who Lived

The street dies, the wrong family is judged, the night Voldemort fell is told, and the baby is left alone when the lamps return.

### Page 1 — The street dies

#### C1-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The street is ordinary and about to stop being so.
- **Image:** A wide panel. The cat is small on the left, so the empty street feels larger than any person.
- **Setting:** Privet Drive, a few minutes before midnight. Wet pavement, a row of identical two-story brick houses, trimmed hedges, one parked car, one upstairs window still yellow. Fog sits at knee height. The sky is clouded.
- **Who:** No people. A thin tabby cat with spectacle-like marks around its eyes sits on a low garden wall. Its fur is damp. It wears nothing and holds nothing.
- **Faces:** The cat stares down the street, ears forward, perfectly still, waiting.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Dumbledore puts out a street lamp with the Deluminator.
- **Image:** A tall figure at the left edge. The silver lighter-shaped Deluminator is in his hand, aimed at the nearest street lamp. A thin ribbon of warm yellow light runs from that lamp’s glass globe into the device, with a few sparks along the ribbon. That lamp is going dark. Lamps he has already passed are out. Lamps farther ahead may still be lit, so the street is dying in one direction. No line from a wand. Do not flood the panel with glow.
- **Setting:** The same wet street, Privet Drive at night. Fog at knee height, identical brick houses. Puddles hold the last of the lamp-light as it leaves.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, very tall, silver hair and beard, half-moon spectacles, indigo-purple star-patterned robes, tall plum hat, plum cloak. The Deluminator is in his hand. His wand stays unused. The thin brown tabby is a small shape on the wall.
- **Faces:** His eyes and his hand are on the lamp he is clicking. He does not look at the houses. The cat may watch.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P1-P3

- **Beat:** The cat watches the last lamp die as its light is pulled out.
- **Image:** Tight on the cat’s face. The last lamp is a small bright coin in each eye, then half gone. That warm light is being pulled out of the globe, toward Dumbledore, who stands out of frame. The lamp is going dark.
- **Setting:** The garden wall, wet brick, a few pale flowers. The street behind is now mostly black. A hedge cuts across the background.
- **Who:** Only the tabby. Whiskers beaded with mist. No collar.
- **Faces:** Eyes wide and knowing, not a pet’s look. It stares past the lamp, toward where Dumbledore stands out of frame.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Dumbledore notices the cat.
- **Image:** Dumbledore has turned. He looks straight at a thin brown tabby on the garden wall. The cat looks back. This is the moment he recognizes her, not a later conversation.
- **Setting:** The wet street, lamps already out, fog, dark brick houses. The wall is low. No other people.
- **Who:** Dumbledore in indigo-purple star robes, plum hat, half-moon spectacles, silver hair and beard. The Deluminator is lowered. His wand is not in use. The cat is Professor McGonagall still in cat form: a thin brown tabby, spectacle-like marks around the eyes, no collar.
- **Faces:** He looks at the cat. The cat looks at him, ears forward, knowing, perfectly still.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "I should have known that you would be here, Professor McGonagall." **McGonagall:** "Good evening, Professor Dumbledore."

#### C1-P1-P5

- **Beat:** The cat becomes McGonagall.
- **Image:** Mid-transformation, or just after: the cat has dropped from the wall and a severe woman stands where it landed, robes still settling.
- **Setting:** The pavement in front of number four. The wall she left is empty. Dark houses, extinguished lamps, fog around her hem.
- **Who:** Minerva McGonagall, sharp face, dark hair in a tight bun, square spectacles, emerald robes, pointed hat. She holds nothing. A few tabby-colored shadows fade off her cloak.
- **Faces:** She looks up at Dumbledore, stern and worried, mouth already open to argue. Her eyes are the same watchful eyes as the cat’s.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Professor Dumbledore. I have been a cat on that wall since morning."

#### C1-P1-P6

- **Beat:** She has been watching this house all day.
- **Image:** Two-shot. McGonagall on the left, Dumbledore taller on the right, the dark front door of the Dursley house centered between them like a third character.
- **Setting:** The doorstep of number four. Neat doormat, brass number, a milk bottle, hedges. No light in the downstairs windows.
- **Who:** McGonagall in emerald robes and hat, hands clasped tight. Dumbledore in purple, the Deluminator lowered, no ribbon and no flame. The street lamps are already out. Neither holds the baby yet.
- **Faces:** She looks at him, exasperated and afraid. He looks at the door, gentle and already decided.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "The Dursleys. Muggles of the worst sort." **Dumbledore:** "And yet the boy must be left at number four, Privet Drive."

### Page 2 — The wrong family

#### C1-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Inside, the Dursleys sleep through the most important night of their street.
- **Image:** A view through the front window, as if from the garden. The glass is a little dirty. Inside, a cluttered sitting room.
- **Setting:** The Dursley sitting room. A large television, too many framed photos of a blond baby, a floral sofa, a lamp Vernon has fallen asleep under. Warm ugly light against the cold street.
- **Who:** Vernon asleep in an armchair, brown suit, mustache, mouth open, a newspaper sliding off his stomach. Petunia asleep sitting up in a mint housedress, hair still pinned. Baby Dudley in a frilly crib at the side, blond and round, one fist in his mouth. No Harry.
- **Faces:** All three are slack with sleep. None of them look toward the window.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P2-P2

- **Beat:** McGonagall says these are the worst people to raise the boy.
- **Image:** McGonagall in profile, close, the window’s warm light edging her face. Her hand is half-raised toward the glass, not touching it.
- **Setting:** Outside the window. The sleeping room is soft and out of focus behind the glass.
- **Who:** McGonagall, emerald robes damp at the hem, hat shadowing her brow. She holds nothing.
- **Faces:** Disgust and pity mixed. She looks at Vernon, then at the blond baby, as if measuring the wrongness of the room.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "You cannot mean to leave a wizard child with these people. There is not a scrap of kindness in that sitting room."

#### C1-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Dumbledore says fame will be a burden for a child.
- **Image:** Dumbledore from a low angle, beard and hat against the black sky, one hand open as he explains.
- **Setting:** The dark garden. The house is a dim block behind him. A few stars show where the cloud has torn.
- **Who:** Dumbledore in purple star robes and plum cloak. The silver device is pocketed. His other hand rests on his wand, unused.
- **Faces:** Sad, kind, looking slightly down at McGonagall out of frame. He is not smiling.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "He will be famous, Professor McGonagall. Books will be written about him. Fame is a cruel weight for a child who cannot yet speak."

#### C1-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Something heavy is coming down out of the cloud.
- **Image:** Both of them small at the bottom of the panel, heads tilted up. Most of the frame is sky.
- **Setting:** Privet Drive rooftops, chimneys, television aerials. A bulge in the cloud, and a distant engine-rumble suggested by shaking leaves.
- **Who:** Dumbledore and McGonagall, full robes, standing close but not touching. The cat is gone.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks alarmed, eyes up. Dumbledore looks up with recognition, almost relief.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "What in Merlin's name is that?" **Dumbledore:** "Hagrid. I asked him to bring the child."

#### C1-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A flying motorcycle drops out of the sky.
- **Image:** The motorcycle large and awkward in the air, too big for the street, headlamp cutting fog. It is just about to touch down.
- **Setting:** Above the Dursley hedge. Houses tilt with the angle. Wet leaves blow outward.
- **Who:** Hagrid enormous on the bike, wild hair and beard streaming, brown overcoat, boots on the pegs. A bundled green blanket is cradled in one huge arm against his chest. His other hand grips the handlebar. The pink umbrella is stuck through a coat pocket.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks wrecked, eyes red, staring at the doorstep he is aiming for, not at the two wizards.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Hagrid lands with the baby.
- **Image:** The motorcycle has stopped, kickstand down, engine smoke. Hagrid fills the center, the bundle tiny against his coat. Dumbledore and McGonagall step in from either side.
- **Setting:** The Dursley drive. Crushed flowers. The headlamp throws long shadows up the brick wall. Neighbors’ windows stay dark.
- **Who:** Hagrid in the moleskin coat, beard wet, holding the green bundle with both hands now as if it might break. Dumbledore reaches, not quite taking the baby. McGonagall’s hands are pressed together.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Dumbledore, begging and grieving at once. Tears track into his beard. Dumbledore looks at the bundle. McGonagall looks at Hagrid’s face, softened.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Professor Dumbledore, sir. Professor McGonagall. I've got little Harry."

### Page 3 — The night Voldemort fell

#### C1-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid says he got the baby out, and his voice breaks.
- **Image:** Close three-quarter view of Hagrid’s face and the top of the baby’s black hair. The panel is tight so his size becomes tenderness.
- **Setting:** The headlamp glow, out of focus hedge, no other detail. Night.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat collar up, a leaf caught in his beard. He holds the baby against his chest. One huge thumb rests near the blanket fold, not on the scar.
- **Faces:** He looks down at Harry, mouth crumpled, trying to speak and failing. Eyes shining.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "I got him out of the house. I got him out, but James and Lily..."

#### C1-P3-P2

- **Beat:** A flash of the ruined cottage and a green light.
- **Image:** A narrow inset memory, jagged edges, not the present street. A small stone cottage with the roof blown open. A spear of green light in a doorway. Two still shapes on the floor, not gory, just fallen. A crib beyond them.
- **Setting:** Godric’s Hollow cottage at night, shown as a memory, colder and greener than Privet Drive. Broken glass. A toy on the step.
- **Who:** No living faces clearly. James is face-down, the same man as the Mirror: late twenties, messy black hair, round glasses knocked aside, the same simple dark jacket, no beard. Lily is collapsed by the crib, the same woman: long straight dark-red hair, green eyes if any show, the same soft pale-green dress, one arm still reaching. They are fallen, not standing, and they do not swap into new clothes. Baby Harry is only a small shape in the blankets. Voldemort is not shown, only the green light.
- **Faces:** None readable. The reaching hand is the emotion.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P3-P3

- **Beat:** He names James and Lily as dead.
- **Image:** Hagrid’s mouth open mid-word, and McGonagall’s eyes closing as she hears the names.
- **Setting:** Still the drive. The motorcycle ticks as it cools behind them. The front door stays shut.
- **Who:** Hagrid holding Harry. McGonagall close, one hand lifted as if to touch the blanket and stopping. Dumbledore slightly behind, hat brim low.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at McGonagall, wrecked. She looks at the baby, eyes wet, mouth tight so she will not cry. Dumbledore looks at neither of them; he looks into the dark.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "They're dead. Both of them. James and Lily Potter."

#### C1-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort killed them and tried to kill the baby.
- **Image:** Hagrid hunches, as if the name itself is heavy. The baby’s blanket is bright green against the brown coat.
- **Setting:** Same drive, tighter crop. Fog around their knees. A neighbor’s cat is not there; the street is empty.
- **Who:** Hagrid, beard hiding half his mouth. He holds Harry more tightly. Dumbledore’s hand enters the frame, palm down, a calming gesture, not a spell.
- **Faces:** Hagrid flinches around the name, eyes squeezed, looking at Dumbledore’s hand rather than saying the word toward the baby. Dumbledore’s visible eye is steady and sad.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "It was You-Know-Who. He killed them. And he tried for the baby an' all." **Dumbledore:** "Say the name, Hagrid. Voldemort failed."

#### C1-P3-P5

- **Beat:** The blanket is folded back. The scar.
- **Image:** The panel is mostly the baby’s face. The lightning cut is the sharpest line in the picture. Adult hands are only fingers at the edges.
- **Setting:** No room, no street. Dark purple sleeve on one side, brown coat on the other, emerald cloth at the bottom. The baby floats in a pool of lamplight.
- **Who:** Baby Harry, black hair, open dark eyes, green blanket. Dumbledore’s fingers hold the fold. Hagrid’s thumb is still there, huge beside the small cheek.
- **Faces:** Harry looks straight out, calm, not crying. Dumbledore, only partly seen, looks at the scar with sorrow and recognition. Hagrid cannot look at the cut; his eyes are on the baby’s eyes.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "See the scar. A lightning cut. That is where the curse struck."

#### C1-P3-P6

- **Beat:** This is the boy who lived. Hagrid sobs.
- **Image:** McGonagall in close profile whispering, and Hagrid behind her with his face buried in one hand. The baby is between them, small and awake.
- **Setting:** The doorstep is just coming into the bottom of the frame. The motorcycle headlamp halos their hair.
- **Who:** McGonagall, hat off now and held against her chest in one hand, the other hand hovering near Harry. Hagrid, shoulders shaking, overcoat creaking. Harry in the green blanket, held by Dumbledore at last.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Harry, awe under the sternness. Hagrid looks at no one, eyes shut, crying. Harry looks at the flame-less dark.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "So this is the boy who lived." **Hagrid:** "Aye. And he's only a baby."

### Page 4 — The letter and the lights

#### C1-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid begs them not to leave the child here.
- **Image:** Hagrid on his knees so his face is level with Dumbledore’s chest, one hand open toward the house, the other reaching back toward the baby.
- **Setting:** The doorstep, milk bottle, brass number, dark windows. The kneeling giant makes the tidy entrance look cruel and small.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat puddled around his knees, beard wet. Dumbledore stands holding Harry. McGonagall stands to the side, hat back on, arms folded hard against her own agreement.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks up at Dumbledore, pleading, no shame in it. Dumbledore looks down at him with love and refusal. McGonagall looks at the door, angry that she agrees.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Don't leave him here, sir. Please. Anywhere but with them."

#### C1-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Harry must grow up away from the stories.
- **Image:** Dumbledore standing straight, the baby tucked in the crook of one arm, the other hand resting on Hagrid’s huge shoulder.
- **Setting:** Night garden, fog, the motorcycle a dark shape. The house looms.
- **Who:** Dumbledore in full purple, Harry’s green blanket, Hagrid kneeling. McGonagall slightly behind, watching the windows in case a light comes on.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks at Hagrid, gentle and final. Hagrid looks at the baby, giving up. Harry’s eyes are on Dumbledore’s beard.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "He must grow up away from our world, Hagrid. Away from the stories, until he is old enough to bear them."

#### C1-P4-P3

- **Beat:** A letter is tucked into the blankets.
- **Image:** Close on Dumbledore’s hand sliding a thick parchment envelope into the green folds, beside the baby’s fist.
- **Setting:** Only blanket, skin, and parchment. The address line is visible as handwriting, not necessary to read. A wax seal catches light.
- **Who:** Dumbledore’s hand, purple cuff with a silver star. Baby Harry’s tiny hand, relaxed. No faces except Harry’s cheek and scar at the edge.
- **Faces:** Harry does not react. The hand is careful, almost reluctant.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P4-P4

- **Beat:** The bundle is set on the doorstep. Harry’s eyes are open.
- **Image:** Low, from the doormat. The baby is small against the big black door. The letter peeks from the blanket. Three pairs of feet stand back.
- **Setting:** The Dursley doorstep. Brass letterbox, doormat, a crack of dark hall under the door. Fog curls over the step.
- **Who:** Baby Harry alone in the green blanket, scar visible, eyes open. At the edges: Dumbledore’s pointed shoes and robe hem, McGonagall’s boots, Hagrid’s enormous boots. No one touches him now.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up, calm, toward the people leaving. We see only their feet, so his gaze feels unanswered.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P4-P5

- **Beat:** They leave in three directions.
- **Image:** A high wide panel. The baby is a tiny green mark on the step. Three figures walk away: the motorcycle lifting, a cat on a wall again, a tall man with a cloak becoming part of the dark.
- **Setting:** The whole street, lamps still out, roofs, hedges, fog. Dawn is not coming yet.
- **Who:** Hagrid astride the flying motorcycle, coat flapping, not looking back. McGonagall as the tabby, tail low, glancing once over her shoulder. Dumbledore walking, hat tall, shoulders bowed. Harry remains on the step.
- **Faces:** Small in the frame. Hagrid’s back is all we see. The cat’s eyes flash once. Dumbledore’s face is hidden by hat brim and dark.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C1-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The stored light returns from the Deluminator. The lamps flare on. The baby lies alone.
- **Image:** The page-turn panel. The same silver Deluminator is in his hand at the far corner. Thin ribbons of warm yellow run from the device up into the street-lamp globes, the reverse of the ribbon that emptied them, and the lamps flare on. Harry is centered in that yellow light on the step, very small, very awake. McGonagall and Hagrid are gone. No wand line. Do not flood the panel with glow.
- **Setting:** Privet Drive restored to ordinary night. Wet pavement shines. The upstairs window is dark now. A milk float is not here yet; it is still deep night. The door has not opened.
- **Who:** Baby Harry, green blanket, black hair, lightning scar, letter tucked beside him. He holds nothing. Dumbledore is small at the far corner, the same hand on the same device, already leaving. No other adults remain.
- **Faces:** Harry looks toward the street, eyes open, not crying. Dumbledore’s face is lost in the dark at the edge. The light is warm and the picture is lonely.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

---

## Chapter 2 — Harry’s life with the Dursleys

Years later. The cupboard, the zoo, the letters, and the flight to the hut.

### Page 1 — The cupboard

#### C2-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Harry wakes in the cupboard under the stairs.
- **Image:** A small hand pushes the cupboard door from inside. A triangle of dusty light cuts across spiders and a thin mattress.
- **Setting:** The cupboard under the Dursley stairs. One bare bulb, unlit. Cobwebs, a few taped drawings, a coat hook with no coat, the sloped underside of the staircase. The hall carpet is just visible outside, clean and bright.
- **Who:** Only Harry’s hand and a slice of his face so far: messy black hair, the edge of taped round glasses, age about ten. He wears a stretched gray T-shirt. He holds the inside of the door.
- **Faces:** Sleepy, wary, looking out into the hall light as if checking whether it is safe.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C2-P1-P2

- **Beat:** He sits up under the stairs.
- **Image:** Harry small inside the cramped cupboard, knees up, the ceiling too low for him to stand. The open door frames a normal house he does not belong to.
- **Setting:** Mattress on the floor, thin blanket, a spider on the bulb. In the hall beyond: polished banister, Dudley’s new trainers kicked off, a smell of bacon suggested by a bright kitchen doorway.
- **Who:** Harry, thin, black hair flat on one side, lightning scar half-hidden by hair, round glasses with a bit of tape on the bridge. Oversized gray shirt, rolled jeans. He holds the blanket in his lap. No shoes yet.
- **Faces:** He looks at his own knees, resigned, not surprised. Mouth small. This is an ordinary morning for him.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C2-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Dudley counts a mountain of birthday presents.
- **Image:** The sitting room is buried in bright wrapping paper. Dudley stands on the sofa to see over the pile. Harry is a thin silhouette in the doorway, easy to miss.
- **Setting:** Morning. Balloons, a banner, too many toys half-unwrapped, Vernon’s newspaper, Petunia’s camera. Sun through net curtains.
- **Who:** Dudley, blond, round, striped polo and new trousers, hugging a video-game box and a toy rifle, standing on the cushions. Vernon behind him, mustache, proud, brown weekend sweater. Petunia clapping, mint dress. Harry at the edge in castoffs, holding nothing, barefoot.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks at the next present, greedy and delighted. Vernon looks at Dudley, beaming. Petunia looks at Dudley. Harry looks at the pile, blank, already knowing none of it is his.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Thirty-six! Count them, Dad, thirty-six presents!" **Vernon:** "And a fine boy deserves every one."

#### C2-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Toast for Harry, a feast for Dudley.
- **Image:** The kitchen table split by the frame. Left side: one plate, one dry slice. Right side: eggs, bacon, fried potatoes, a stack of toast, ketchup.
- **Setting:** A spotless suburban kitchen. Checked curtains, a fruit bowl Dudley will not eat, a calendar with Dudley’s school photo. Morning light.
- **Who:** Harry sits on the left in the gray shirt and rolled jeans, hands in his lap, not reaching yet. Dudley on the right, already chewing, striped shirt stained with egg. Petunia stands behind Dudley with a frying pan, apron on, serving him more. Vernon sits at the head with tea and the paper.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his toast, not at them. Dudley looks at Harry’s plate only to make sure it is smaller, smug. Petunia looks at Dudley’s plate, anxious it might not be enough. Vernon looks at the paper, ignoring Harry on purpose.
- **Dialogue:** **Petunia:** "Eat your toast. And don't you stare at Dudley's plate."

#### C2-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Vernon tells Harry not to ask questions.
- **Image:** Vernon lowers the paper just enough to show his eyes and mustache. One thick finger points at Harry. The paper still hides the rest of him.
- **Setting:** The same kitchen. Dudley’s feast in the foreground, soft. A wall clock. The cupboard door is visible down the hall, ajar.
- **Who:** Vernon in a shirt and tie even at breakfast, mustache bristling, finger pointed. Harry across the table, small, glasses, hands flat on either side of the single toast, wearing the same castoffs.
- **Faces:** Vernon glares over the paper at Harry, warning. Harry looks back, cautious, mouth closed. He has learned not to answer with a question.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "No questions. We do not speak of that rubbish in this house, boy."

#### C2-P1-P6

- **Beat:** The family photo has no Harry in it.
- **Image:** A framed photo fills the panel. Four edges of a smiling blond family. At the bottom corner, Harry’s reflection is faint in the glass, outside the picture.
- **Setting:** The wall above the sofa. Other frames, all Dudley. Floral wallpaper. The room behind the reflection is the sitting room we just saw.
- **Who:** Inside the photo: Vernon, Petunia, and Dudley in matching sweaters, grinning at the camera. In the glass reflection only: Harry, messy hair, taped glasses, gray shirt, looking up at the frame. He holds nothing.
- **Faces:** The photo-family looks delighted and false. Harry’s reflection looks quiet and hurt, eyes on the place where he should be standing.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 2 — The zoo

#### C2-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Dudley bangs on the snake’s glass.
- **Image:** Dudley’s palms and the toy-sticky fingerprints on thick glass. Behind the glass, a large snake coiled, unimpressed. Harry stands a step behind, small.
- **Setting:** The reptile house. Greenish light, other tanks receding into dark, a sign, echoing tile, a few bored visitors. Humid air suggested by fogged glass. One enclosure, one snake, the same animal in every zoo panel.
- **Who:** Dudley in a bright puffy jacket, striped shirt, both hands hammering the glass, face squashed close. Harry behind him in a donated windcheater too big, jeans, taped glasses, hands in pockets. The snake is the zoo snake: one large green-brown boa with darker saddle blotches, thick-bodied, longer than a child is tall, coiled so it fills the enclosure floor, the same broad boa head, the same pale gold eye, tongue flicking. Not a cobra and not a smaller snake. Vernon and Petunia are soft shapes further down the hall, watching Dudley, not the animals. Vernon is the early-chapter man: large, beefy, very little neck, big black mustache, brown coat.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks at the snake, impatient, wanting it to do a trick. The snake looks past him. Harry looks at the snake, curious and gentle.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Make it move! It's a boring snake!"

#### C2-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The snake ignores Dudley and looks at Harry.
- **Image:** Through the glass, the same snake’s eye, large and pale gold, aimed past Dudley’s ear toward Harry. Dudley is a blur at the edge. The head is the same broad boa head as the panel before, not a new species in close-up.
- **Setting:** Inside the tank: rock, branch, heat-lamp glow, shed skin in the corner. The visitor side is dim. The body filling the floor is still the green-brown boa with darker saddles.
- **Who:** The zoo snake, head raised, same size relative to the glass as in the wide panel, same pale gold eye, same saddle pattern. Dudley’s ear and yelling mouth out of focus. Harry sharp in the background of the reflection, windcheater, glasses, scar just visible under his hair.
- **Faces:** The snake looks only at Harry, calm, attentive. Dudley looks at the glass, frustrated, not understanding he has been dismissed. Harry looks back, startled and pleased.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C2-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Harry speaks to the snake, and it answers by moving.
- **Image:** Harry close to the glass, mouth slightly open, one hand spread on the pane. The snake has slid nearer, tongue almost touching the spot opposite his fingers.
- **Setting:** The same tank. Other visitors have drifted off. A bench. The sign is unreadable on purpose. Green light on Harry’s face. The enclosure is still the one that this snake fills.
- **Who:** Harry, windcheater unzipped, gray shirt, taped glasses, hair messy from the day. Palm on the glass. He holds nothing else. The same zoo snake, green-brown with darker saddles, coiled up to meet him, head the same shape, pale gold eye, body still longer than he is tall. Not a pet-shop snake. Dudley is gone from this panel, off to complain.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the snake’s eye, earnest, as if talking to someone who has also been stuck in a small room. The snake looks back, steady. No fear on either side.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Sorry about him. You get tired of being stuck behind glass, don't you."

#### C2-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Dudley shoves Harry aside. The glass is gone.
- **Image:** Dudley’s shoulder hits Harry. Harry stumbles. The glass that was just there is missing, a clean absence, the snake’s side of the world suddenly open to the hall.
- **Setting:** Reptile house tile, the tank rim, Dudley’s fingerprints still hanging in the air where the glass was, a visual trick of smears with no pane. Alarmed visitors start to turn. The enclosure floor is the one the same boa was filling.
- **Who:** Dudley in the bright jacket, both hands grabbing for the spot Harry stood, leaning in. Harry falling sideways, one hand still out. The same zoo snake beginning to pour over the rim: green-brown, darker saddles, thick body, the same head, the same pale gold eye, still longer than Dudley is tall. Not a sudden giant and not a thin snake. Vernon in the deep background, the early-chapter man, big black mustache, very little neck, brown coat, starting to notice.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks where the snake should be performing, confused. Harry looks at the empty frame, shocked. The snake looks at the open hall, suddenly awake.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Mum! The glass has gone!"

#### C2-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Dudley falls in. The snake slides past Harry.
- **Image:** Dudley topples into the tank, legs up, bright jacket bunched. The same snake flows across the tile past Harry’s sneakers, unhurried, toward a side door of daylight. Its body is still the thick green-brown boa, not a new shining serpent.
- **Setting:** The reptile house in chaos. A keeper distant, visitors backing up. The open tank is a pit of rock and branch, the same enclosure. Daylight leaks from an exit sign.
- **Who:** Dudley upside down in the tank, face red, hands grabbing rock. Harry standing very still at the rim, windcheater, glasses crooked, both hands slightly raised, not grabbing the snake. The zoo snake, same individual, green-brown with darker saddles, same head, same pale gold eye, thick body longer than the child in the tank, passing his shoes. Petunia in the background, hands on her cheeks, mint coat.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks up at Harry, furious and scared. Harry looks down at the snake, almost kind, a goodbye. The snake does not look back. Petunia looks at Dudley, horrified.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Get me out! Harry did it!"

#### C2-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Vernon drags Harry out through the crowd.
- **Image:** Vernon’s fist in the back of Harry’s windcheater, Harry half-lifted, feet skidding. The crowd parts. Dudley is a wailing shape behind them, held by Petunia.
- **Setting:** The zoo exit. Bright posters, a turnstile, daylight slamming in after the green dark. People stare.
- **Who:** Vernon, face purple, mustache bristling, brown coat, one hand crushing Harry’s collar, the other stabbing a finger back toward the reptile house. Harry, glasses askew, windcheater yanked up to his ears, scar bright, arms pinwheeled. Petunia fussing over Dudley, who is wet and soiled from the tank, jacket ruined.
- **Faces:** Vernon glares down at Harry, blaming him, not the missing glass. Harry looks ahead, not at Vernon, jaw tight, embarrassed and angry. Strangers look at Harry, uneasy, as if they saw something they cannot name.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "You did that on purpose. You and that snake. Walk."

### Page 3 — The letters

#### C2-P3-P1

- **Beat:** An owl drops a letter onto the eggs.
- **Image:** Breakfast chaos frozen. A tawny owl is just leaving the window, wings spread. A thick parchment envelope has landed in the frying pan, against the eggs. Vernon’s fork is stopped halfway.
- **Setting:** The Dursley kitchen, morning. Open window, net curtain blowing, suburban gardens outside. The table is set. The hall and the cupboard are visible behind Harry.
- **Who:** The owl, ordinary feathers, a bit grand, already turning away. Vernon in shirt and tie, fork in hand, mustache. Petunia with a spatula. Dudley with a mouthful. Harry in the gray shirt and rolled jeans, half out of his chair, eyes on the envelope. The letter is addressed in green ink; the line about the cupboard is visible as shapes, not a quote.
- **Faces:** Vernon stares at the owl, outraged. Petunia stares at the letter, already afraid. Dudley looks at the owl, delighted by a new toy. Harry looks at the letter, hungry, the first real hope on his face in this chapter.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Dad, an owl! A real owl in the kitchen!"

#### C2-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Vernon snatches the letter and goes white.
- **Image:** The letter is in Vernon’s fist, crumpled. Harry’s hand is still open where the letter was. Vernon’s face has lost all its purple.
- **Setting:** Tight on the table. Eggs, toast, the owl gone. The window bangs.
- **Who:** Vernon, shirt and tie, both hands crushing the parchment, knuckles white. Harry reaching across, thin wrist, taped glasses, gray sleeve. Dudley and Petunia are cropped out except Dudley’s shocked eye at the edge.
- **Faces:** Vernon looks at the address, not at Harry, blood drained, afraid. Harry looks at the letter in Vernon’s hands, desperate. His mouth is open on a question he is not allowed to ask.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "Give me that. You will not read it." **Harry:** "It's addressed to me. To the cupboard under the stairs."

#### C2-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Petunia reads over his shoulder and whispers her sister’s name.
- **Image:** Petunia bent over Vernon’s shoulder, lips at his ear. The letter is open now. A crest is visible, not lingered on. Her eyes are far away.
- **Setting:** The kitchen has gone still. Dudley is a soft blob in the background holding a sausage. The clock is the only sharp background object.
- **Who:** Petunia, apron, hair too tight, one floury hand on Vernon’s shoulder. Vernon seated, letter open, mustache drooping. Harry standing now, small, watching them, hands empty.
- **Faces:** Petunia looks at the letter but sees a memory; her mouth shapes a name she hates and misses. Vernon looks up at her, lost. Harry looks at Petunia, seeing a crack in her for the first time.
- **Dialogue:** **Petunia:** "Lily. It's from her sort. I knew this day would come."

#### C2-P3-P4

- **Beat:** The next day, three letters. Vernon nails the mail slot shut.
- **Image:** Vernon on his knees at the front door, hammer raised, nails in his teeth. Three thick letters are already halfway through the slot, pushing. Harry watches from the stairs.
- **Setting:** The Dursley hall. Doormat, Vernon’s shoes, the cupboard door under the stairs. Daylight through the door glass. More owl shadows cross that glass.
- **Who:** Vernon in rolled shirtsleeves, tie thrown over his shoulder, hammer in one hand, nails in his mouth, sweat on his balding head. Harry on the stairs in castoffs, one hand on the banister. Dudley peeks from the kitchen, nervous now, not laughing. Petunia wrings a tea towel.
- **Faces:** Vernon looks at the slot with hatred, as if the door is betraying him. Harry looks at the letters, longing. Petunia looks at Harry, guilty and angry. Dudley looks at his father, starting to be scared of him.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "No more letters. Not through this door. Not to any cupboard in my house."

#### C2-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Letters pour down the chimney. Dudley screams.
- **Image:** A flood of envelopes blasting out of the fireplace into the sitting room, dozens, then hundreds. Dudley is backed into a corner, arms over his head. Harry stands in the paper storm, reaching.
- **Setting:** The sitting room. The family photo wall. The television. A fire that has gone out under the paper. Daytime, but the room is full of whirling parchment.
- **Who:** Dudley in the striped polo, crouched, mouth wide. Harry in the gray shirt, hair full of paper, both hands up, trying to catch one letter. Vernon in the doorway with a bucket, useless. Petunia behind him, mint dress, hands in her hair. No owls in the room; the letters arrive by themselves.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks up at the chimney, terrified. Harry looks at a letter just out of reach, lit up, almost laughing. Vernon looks furious and small. Petunia looks at the mess as if her house is being taken.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Make them stop! They're coming down the chimney!"

#### C2-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Vernon boards the fireplace, wild-eyed.
- **Image:** Vernon hammers a plank across the fireplace. Nails spray. Letters bulge behind the wood. His eyes are round and bloodshot. The family photo is crooked behind him.
- **Setting:** The sitting room wrecked with envelopes. Daylight. The boarded hearth is the new center.
- **Who:** Vernon, shirtsleeves filthy, hammer blurred with motion, mustache damp, no tie. Harry at the edge holding one letter he has finally caught, hidden against his stomach. Dudley crying into Petunia’s apron. She holds him and stares.
- **Faces:** Vernon looks over his shoulder at Harry, wild, not at the chimney. Harry looks back, the letter pressed to his shirt, defiant for the first time. Petunia looks at Vernon as if she does not know him. Dudley looks at no one, face buried.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "I will not have magic in this house. Do you hear me, boy?"

### Page 4 — The hut

#### C2-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The car races into a storm. Harry is wedged beside Dudley.
- **Image:** Inside the car, rain smearing the windows. Harry is crushed against the door by Dudley’s bulk and a suitcase. Vernon drives hunched. A letter is stuck to the outside of the glass, riding the storm.
- **Setting:** A country road at night, seen through wet glass. Black hedges, no lights, the wipers losing. The back seat is luggage and boys.
- **Who:** Vernon at the wheel, coat, both hands tight, mustache, eyes on the road. Petunia in the passenger seat, coat belted, clutching her handbag, looking back at the letter on the window. Dudley in a puffy coat, eating a chocolate bar, taking the whole seat. Harry in a thin jacket, glasses fogged, knees up, a suitcase digging into his ribs. He holds the door handle.
- **Faces:** Vernon glares at the road, hunted. Petunia looks at the letter, pale. Dudley looks at his chocolate, annoyed at the trip. Harry looks at the letter on the glass, hopeful even now.
- **Dialogue:** **Petunia:** "There's another one. Stuck to the glass." **Vernon:** "Don't look at it. Drive."

#### C2-P4-P2

- **Beat:** A shack on a rock. Waves hit the walls.
- **Image:** A tiny hut on a rock in black water, the Dursley car parked at a mad angle on the only flat bit. A wave explodes against the wall and whites out half the panel.
- **Setting:** Night storm. No town. One yellow window in the shack. Spray, wind-bent grass, a ruined jetty. The sky is low.
- **Who:** Four small figures run from the car to the door, coats over their heads. Vernon leads with a suitcase. Petunia clutches Dudley. Harry is last, thin jacket plastered to him, glasses in one hand so he will not lose them, the other hand empty.
- **Faces:** Too small for detail, but Harry is the only one looking back at the sky instead of at the door.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C2-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Vernon says nobody will find them here.
- **Image:** Inside the hut, Vernon spreads his arms in victory. The family is miserable around him. Water drips from the ceiling into a pan.
- **Setting:** One room. A cot, a stove, a table, a storm loud outside, a single bare bulb. Wet coats on nails. The fireplace is cold. No letters. Yet.
- **Who:** Vernon in a damp knit vest, mustache smug, arms wide, holding nothing, proud of the room. Petunia on the cot in her coat, hair ruined, handbag in her lap. Dudley on the floor eating the last of the chocolate, puffy coat still on. Harry in the corner on a blanket, thin jacket, glasses back on, scar, knees to chest.
- **Faces:** Vernon looks at his family, expecting thanks. Petunia looks at a drip, exhausted. Dudley looks at his hands, sulking. Harry looks at the dark window, not believing Vernon.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "Let the owls try. Nobody will find us on this rock."

#### C2-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Harry counts seconds to midnight. His birthday.
- **Image:** Harry alone in the corner, lips moving as he counts. A small clock or his fingers: ten fingers spread, then folding. The bulb flickers. Behind him the Dursleys sleep in a heap.
- **Setting:** The hut at night. Storm quieter for a moment. Shadows of the family large on the wall. Harry’s blanket is the thinnest.
- **Who:** Harry, jacket used as a pillow, gray shirt, jeans, glasses on so he will not miss it. He holds up his fingers to count. Vernon snores in the cot, vest unbuttoned. Petunia and Dudley are shapes under coats.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his own fingers, intent, a private birthday. A tiny hopeful pull at his mouth. The others are asleep, faces slack.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Ten. Nine. Happy birthday. Nobody has to know."

#### C2-P4-P5

- **Beat:** The storm holds still. A huge shadow crosses the window.
- **Image:** The window, salt-crusted. A shadow too big to be a man crosses it, shoulders first, then wild hair. The bulb stops flickering. Even the drip seems to pause.
- **Setting:** The hut wall and window. Outside is black sea and a shape that blocks the weather. Inside, sleeping bodies, Harry sitting up.
- **Who:** Harry in the foreground, half-risen, one hand on the floor, glasses flashing. The shadow outside has Hagrid’s beard and bulk, not yet a clear person. The Dursleys still sleep, Vernon’s mustache the only clear feature.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the window, eyes wide, afraid and ready. His mouth is slightly open. No one else is awake to look.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C2-P4-P6

- **Beat:** The door booms. Vernon grabs a rifle. Harry stares.
- **Image:** The page-turn. The hut door bows inward with a boom, dust jumping off the frame. Vernon is on his feet with a hunting rifle aimed at the door. Harry is still on the floor, looking up, not hiding.
- **Setting:** The one-room shack. Bulb swinging. Coats falling off nails. Sea visible through the shaking window. The door’s planks strain.
- **Who:** Vernon in his vest and trousers, barefoot, rifle tucked into his shoulder, mustache fierce, face purple again. Petunia awake, clutching Dudley, both in coats, Dudley’s chocolate smeared. Harry on the blanket, thin, glasses, scar, hands open on his knees, wearing the same castoffs he woke up in.
- **Faces:** Vernon glares at the door, teeth bared. Petunia looks at Vernon, then the door, terrified. Dudley looks at the rifle, crying. Harry looks at the door with recognition he cannot explain yet, not at the gun.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "Who's there? I've got a rifle!"
---

## Chapter 3 — The Keeper of the Keys

The door bursts, Vernon denies magic, the truth about Harry’s parents lands, and Hagrid takes him away.

### Page 1 — The door

#### C3-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The door blasts in. Hagrid fills the frame.
- **Image:** The shack door is off its hinges and falling inward. Beyond it, Hagrid is so large the night sea is only a strip around his shoulders.
- **Setting:** The hut interior, bulb swinging, spray blowing in. The fallen door slams the table. Outside: black water and wind.
- **Who:** Hagrid in the doorway, wild black hair and beard full of rain, brown moleskin overcoat, huge boots, pink umbrella raised like a harmless weapon in one fist. Vernon is a small shape with the rifle at the left, already useless. Harry is on the floor at the bottom of the frame.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks into the room, fierce until his eyes find Harry, then the fierceness cracks. Vernon looks up, mustache shaking. Harry looks up, stunned.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Sorry about the door."

#### C3-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Hagrid wishes Harry a happy birthday and hugs him off his feet.
- **Image:** Harry disappears into the brown coat. Only his sneakers and one arm show. Hagrid’s beard covers the top of Harry’s head.
- **Setting:** The hut, door open, rain slanting in. The Dursleys are pressed against the far wall, small.
- **Who:** Hagrid kneeling now so the hug is possible, overcoat puddled, one huge hand on Harry’s back, the other still holding a slightly squashed cake tin. Harry in the thin jacket, gray shirt, rolled jeans, taped glasses knocked sideways, scar against Hagrid’s coat. He holds nothing; his arms are stuck out in surprise.
- **Faces:** Hagrid’s eyes are shut, crying and smiling into the hug, looking at no one. Harry’s visible eye is wide, looking sideways at the Dursleys, not sure he is allowed to be held.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Happy birthday, Harry."

#### C3-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Vernon shouts. Hagrid bends the rifle into a knot.
- **Image:** The rifle barrel is a loop. Vernon’s hands are still on the stock. Hagrid uses two fingers, not even a grunt.
- **Setting:** Tight on the gun and their hands. The hut wall and a dripping coat behind.
- **Who:** Hagrid’s huge hand, coat cuff, beard at the top edge. Vernon’s softer hands, wedding ring, knit vest, mustache cropped at the top, face purple. The rifle, wood and metal, ruined. Harry is a small watching face behind Hagrid’s arm, glasses, wet hair.
- **Faces:** Vernon stares at the knotted barrel, belief breaking. Hagrid looks at Vernon with mild annoyance, not triumph. Harry looks at the knot, a startled almost-smile.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "I demand that you leave this instant!" **Hagrid:** "Mind the rifle, Mr Dursley. It bends."

#### C3-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Hagrid holds out the soggy letter.
- **Image:** The parchment envelope, thick, green ink, a wax seal, damp at the corners, offered on Hagrid’s palm. It is bigger than Harry’s hand.
- **Setting:** The cake tin is on the table. Rain darkens the doorstep. The Dursleys are a blurred clump.
- **Who:** Hagrid crouched, coat steaming, beard dripping, the letter on his open hand. Harry standing in front of him, thin jacket, jeans, glasses, both hands hovering, not quite touching it. Vernon behind, empty-handed now, the knotted rifle on the floor.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, encouraging, gentle. Harry looks at the letter, hungry and afraid to hope. Vernon looks at the letter with hate.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "This letter's yours. Hogwarts has been trying to reach you for days."

#### C3-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Harry reads the words Hogwarts and witchcraft.
- **Image:** Over Harry’s shoulder. The letter is open. A crest sits at the top. His finger rests under a line. We do not need every word; the school’s name is the clear part.
- **Setting:** The bare bulb lights the parchment. The hut is quiet for one panel. Water drips into the pan.
- **Who:** Harry, hair falling forward, taped glasses, scar, gray sleeves too long. He holds the letter in both hands as if it might fly away. Hagrid’s big face is soft in the background, watching him read.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down at the paper, lips moving, brow tight with confusion and want. Hagrid looks at Harry’s face, waiting for the smile.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hogwarts School of Witchcraft and Wizardry."

#### C3-P1-P6

- **Beat:** He does not know what a wizard is yet.
- **Image:** Harry lowers the letter and looks up. The word is in his mouth and means nothing solid. The panel is his face, letter at the bottom edge.
- **Setting:** The hut wall, coats, the dark door. No magic has been seen except the bent gun.
- **Who:** Harry, ten turning eleven tonight, thin, messy black hair, round taped glasses, lightning scar, thin jacket over the gray shirt. He holds the letter against his chest. Hagrid is a warm blur behind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, lost, asking without a full question. His eyes are wet from weather and from being seen.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "A wizard? I don't even know what that means."

### Page 2 — Denial

#### C3-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Vernon says there is no magic and Harry is not going.
- **Image:** Vernon jabs a finger at Hagrid’s chest and has to reach up to do it. He looks ridiculous and does not know it. Harry holds the letter behind his back.
- **Setting:** The hut. Dudley hides behind Petunia. The storm is louder again.
- **Who:** Vernon, vest, bare feet, mustache, finger pointing, face purple. Hagrid standing again, head near the bulb, coat, umbrella in his belt, arms folded. Harry behind Hagrid’s leg, letter hidden, glasses, jacket. Petunia in her belted coat, both hands on Dudley’s shoulders. Dudley in the puffy coat, chocolate on his chin.
- **Faces:** Vernon glares up at Hagrid, shouting. Hagrid looks down, insulted, not yet angry. Harry looks at Vernon, afraid the letter will be taken. Petunia looks at the letter behind Harry’s back. Dudley looks at Hagrid’s boots, awed.
- **Dialogue:** **Vernon:** "There is no such thing as magic. And you are not going to any school of freaks."

#### C3-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hagrid is offended for Harry and for the school.
- **Image:** Hagrid draws himself up. The bulb swings away from his head. He points the pink umbrella, not firing it, just scolding with it.
- **Setting:** The low ceiling makes him hunch. Rain on the broken door.
- **Who:** Hagrid, beard jutting, overcoat, umbrella in one hand like a teacher’s pointer. Vernon shrinking despite himself. Harry looking up the mountain of coat.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Vernon, genuinely offended, voice big. He keeps glancing down at Harry to make sure the boy hears he is being defended. Vernon looks at the umbrella, scorn cracking into nerves.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Watch your mouth. Hogwarts is the finest school of witchcraft in the world, and Harry's got a place in it."

#### C3-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Dudley laughs and steals the cake.
- **Image:** Dudley’s hand is in the cake tin, frosting on his fingers, mouth open in a laugh. The cake was meant for Harry. Hagrid has not seen yet.
- **Setting:** The shaky table, the tin lid on the floor, storm noise.
- **Who:** Dudley, puffy coat, striped shirt, round face, both hands stealing a chunk of cake. Harry beside the table, letter still held, looking at Dudley in disbelief. Hagrid’s back is turned, talking to Vernon. Petunia sees and does not stop her son.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks at Harry, mocking, cake already at his lips. Harry looks at Dudley, hurt and tired of it. Petunia looks away, choosing not to see.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Ha! His cake's squashed. I'm having it."

#### C3-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Hagrid points the pink umbrella. A spark.
- **Image:** A small pink spark leaps from the umbrella tip. It is not a grand spell. It is a cross adult flicking a naughty child, except the adult is a giant.
- **Setting:** The hut, tight. Cake crumbs in the air. The spark lights everyone’s faces pink for one instant.
- **Who:** Hagrid, umbrella extended, coat, stern. Dudley with cake in his mouth, eyes crossing toward the spark. Harry flinching back, letter hugged tight. Vernon reaching too late. Petunia’s hands flying up.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Dudley, annoyed, not cruel. Dudley looks at the spark, the laugh dying. Harry looks at the spark, wonder breaking through.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "That's enough out of you."

#### C3-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Dudley runs with a curly tail poking through his trousers.
- **Image:** Dudley sprints for the door, both hands on his backside. A pink curly pig tail sticks out through a new hole in his trousers. Cake is still in his mouth.
- **Setting:** The broken doorway, night rain, the rock outside. The tail is the brightest color in a brown room.
- **Who:** Dudley, coat flapping, striped shirt, tail unmistakable. Petunia lunging after him, coat, hairpins falling. Vernon frozen. Harry and Hagrid in the midground, Harry’s letter safe.
- **Faces:** Dudley looks back over his shoulder, howling, eyes huge. Petunia looks at the tail, mind breaking. Harry looks at the tail, a real smile starting. Hagrid looks satisfied and a bit guilty.
- **Dialogue:** **Dudley:** "Mum! There's a tail! I've got a tail!"

#### C3-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Petunia shrieks. Harry almost smiles.
- **Image:** Petunia’s mouth wide, hands in her hair. In the foreground Harry’s smile is small and private, the first one that reaches his eyes. He covers it with the letter.
- **Setting:** The hut in uproar. Dudley is a tail and a wail outside. The bulb still swings.
- **Who:** Petunia, coat open, apron edge, face pale, no dignity left. Harry close to us, glasses, scar, wet hair, letter over his mouth. Hagrid scratching his beard, umbrella lowered. Vernon sitting down hard on the cot.
- **Faces:** Petunia looks at Hagrid, shrieking. Harry looks at the floor, smiling despite himself. Hagrid looks at Harry’s smile and softens. Vernon looks at nothing, defeated.
- **Dialogue:** **Petunia:** "What have you done to my Dudley?"

### Page 3 — The story

#### C3-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid says the car-crash story was a lie.
- **Image:** Hagrid sits on the floor so he can speak quietly. The cake tin is between them. The Dursleys are far in the background, huddled, not part of this conversation.
- **Setting:** The hut, storm dropping. A blanket around Harry’s shoulders now, one of Hagrid’s huge spare cloths. The bulb steady.
- **Who:** Hagrid cross-legged, coat open, beard, hands on his knees, umbrella beside him. Harry on an overturned crate, blanket, letter in his lap, glasses, scar. He holds a chipped mug of tea too big for him.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, gentle and grave. Harry looks at him, guarded, because good news in this house usually is not.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "That story they told you, about a car crash. It's a lie, Harry."

#### C3-P3-P2

- **Beat:** His parents were a witch and a wizard, and they were murdered.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands tighten on the mug. Tea jumps. Hagrid’s big hands open, empty, as he says it.
- **Setting:** Tight two-shot. The Dursleys are out of frame on purpose. Rain on the roof.
- **Who:** Harry, blanket slipping, gray shirt, the letter crushed a little in one hand, mug in the other. Hagrid, sorrow in the set of his shoulders, coat, no smile.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry and does not look away. Harry looks at the tea, then up, the old car-crash story falling off his face. Shock, not tears yet.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Your mum and dad were a witch and a wizard. And they were murdered."

#### C3-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Hagrid lowers his voice to say Voldemort, then flinches.
- **Image:** Hagrid leans in. The name is a small dark space between them, not written on the picture. He winces as if the word burned his tongue. Harry leans in to hear.
- **Setting:** The bulb seems dimmer. Shadows under Hagrid’s brow. The storm is a hush.
- **Who:** The two of them only. Hagrid’s hand half-covers his own mouth after speaking. Harry still holds the mug and the letter.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks sideways, afraid of the name, then back at Harry to be brave for him. Harry looks at Hagrid’s flinch, catching the fear before he catches the meaning.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "It was Voldemort. Most folk won't say it. They call him You-Know-Who."

#### C3-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort tried to kill Harry and could not.
- **Image:** Hagrid points, very gently, toward Harry’s forehead, not touching. The scar is in the light.
- **Setting:** Same corner of the hut. Petunia is a sharp, listening silhouette far behind, the only Dursley still paying attention.
- **Who:** Hagrid, one finger raised, coat, beard. Harry, scar exposed because his hair is wet and pushed back, glasses, blanket. Petunia in the deep background, coat, face half-lit, listening.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the scar with reverence and grief. Harry looks at Hagrid’s finger, then touches his own scar. Petunia looks at the scar too, and for once she is not sneering.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "He tried the Killing Curse on you, and it didn't take. That's why you've got the lightning scar."

#### C3-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Harry touches his scar.
- **Image:** Close on Harry’s fingers on the lightning scar. His eyes are above, out of focus, filling.
- **Setting:** No hut. Just skin, hair, the metal of his glasses, the edge of the blanket.
- **Who:** Harry only. Thin fingers, a child’s hand, the scar a pale raised line. He holds nothing in this crop.
- **Faces:** He looks through the reader, remembering a pain he was told was a car crash. Confusion, anger, and the start of grief.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "This? They told me the crash did it."

#### C3-P3-P6

- **Beat:** That is why every witch and wizard knows Harry’s name.
- **Image:** Hagrid tries a smile that does not quite work. Harry looks smaller than ever, wrapped in the blanket, the letter in his lap like a ticket he did not buy.
- **Setting:** The hut door open a crack. A strip of calmer sea. The Dursleys silent.
- **Who:** Hagrid, hands spread, explaining fame as if it were a comfort. Harry, blanket, glasses, letter, mug forgotten on the crate. Petunia’s mouth is a thin line in the background.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, proud of him and sorry. Harry looks at his own name on the letter, overwhelmed, not proud. He does not look at Hagrid yet.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Every witch and wizard alive knows the name Harry Potter. You're the boy who lived."

### Page 4 — Leaving

#### C3-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Petunia says she knew. She hated her sister for being special.
- **Image:** Petunia stands, suddenly the sharpest person in the room. Years of spite are in her posture. Harry turns as if slapped.
- **Setting:** The hut. Dawn is a gray line at the window. Coats, the knotted rifle, Dudley asleep at last on the cot.
- **Who:** Petunia, coat belted, hair pinned again by habit, handbag on her arm, empty hands clenched. Harry turned on the crate, blanket falling. Hagrid half-risen, warning. Vernon seated, looking at the floor, mustache limp. Dudley asleep, tail hidden under a coat.
- **Faces:** Petunia looks at Harry and sees Lily. Her face is ugly with envy and an old wound. Harry looks at her, shocked. Hagrid looks at Petunia, angry on Lily’s behalf.
- **Dialogue:** **Petunia:** "I knew. My sister Lily was a witch. She got the magic, and I got nothing. I hated her for being special."

#### C3-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Harry looks at her as if seeing her for the first time.
- **Image:** A clean two-shot. Aunt and nephew. No one between them. The family resemblance is in the set of the mouth and nowhere else.
- **Setting:** Gray dawn light from the side, kinder than the bulb. The shack is shabby.
- **Who:** Petunia, thin, long neck, mint collar under the coat, nothing in her hands. Harry, small, scar, taped glasses, blanket around his shoulders like a cloak he has not earned yet. The letter is in his fist.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at her, really looks, hurt and curious. Petunia looks just past him, because looking at his eyes is too much like looking at her sister. Her mouth is hard.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "You knew what I was. All this time, and you let me sleep in a cupboard."

#### C3-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hagrid says they are leaving to buy Harry’s school things.
- **Image:** Hagrid’s hand is offered, big enough for Harry to stand on. The door is open. Day is outside. The Dursleys are already the past, small in the room behind.
- **Setting:** Doorway of the shack, wet rock, a calmer sea, the flying motorcycle waiting, absurd and wonderful.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat, umbrella, the cake tin tucked under one arm, other hand out. Harry standing, blanket still on, letter clutched, jacket, jeans, sneakers. He is about to take the hand. Vernon, Petunia, and sleeping Dudley remain in the dark interior.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, sure and kind. Harry looks at the hand, then past it at the sky. Vernon looks at the motorcycle, blank. Petunia looks at Harry’s back.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Come on. We've school things to buy. Robes, books, a wand."

#### C3-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Harry asks if he can really be a wizard. Hagrid says of course he is.
- **Image:** They are in the doorway together. Hagrid laughs, a real laugh, the first warm sound in the chapter. Harry’s shoulders drop.
- **Setting:** Threshold. Inside is brown and small. Outside is silver water and cloud breaking.
- **Who:** Harry looking up, letter against his chest, blanket, glasses. Hagrid looking down, beard shaking with the laugh, one hand on Harry’s shoulder, not pushing.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, wanting the answer to be simple. Hope is naked on his face. Hagrid looks at him as if the question is silly and lovely. Of course.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Can I really be a wizard?" **Hagrid:** "Course you can. You are one."

#### C3-P4-P5

- **Beat:** They step into the rain. Harry looks back once.
- **Image:** From inside the hut, looking out. Harry and Hagrid walk onto the rock. Harry’s head is turned. The Dursleys are shapes at the edge of the interior, not followed.
- **Setting:** Wet stone, spray, the motorcycle large and shining with rain. The shack doorway frames the shot like a mouth letting him go.
- **Who:** Harry, thin jacket soaked again, letter inside his shirt now, one hand on Hagrid’s coat so he will not slip. Hagrid ahead, huge, cake tin, umbrella. Behind, only Petunia’s face in the dark, watching.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back, not waving, not forgiving, just marking the place. His face is serious. Petunia looks at him, unreadable. Hagrid does not look back.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C3-P4-P6

- **Beat:** A giant and a boy walk toward a city glow.
- **Image:** Wide page-turn. The rock and shack are tiny behind them. Ahead, far off, a dirty gold glow of London under the clouds. They are two silhouettes, one enormous, one small, walking.
- **Setting:** Coastal path at dawn. Grass, a road, puddles reflecting the distant city. Gulls. The storm is behind them, literally in the sky over the shack.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat flapping, umbrella, tin. Harry beside him, blanket worn like a cape, sneakers, letter safe against his chest. No Dursleys.
- **Faces:** Seen mostly from behind. Harry’s profile shows as he looks up at Hagrid, the hope from the last panel still there. Hagrid looks ahead, toward the city, at ease.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

---

## Chapter 4 — Diagon Alley

The hidden street, the bank, the supplies, the wand, and Draco.

### Page 1 — The pub and the wall

#### C4-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Ordinary people walk past the Leaky Cauldron and do not see it.
- **Setting:** A cramped London back street, daytime, bins, a launderette, pigeons. The pub is squeezed between a bookshop and a record shop, its sign so old the letters flake. Muggle pedestrians pass with shopping bags and briefcases.
- **Image:** The pub is painted a little sharper than the street, as if the panel knows it is there and the pedestrians do not. Their eyelines slide off it.
- **Who:** Hagrid and Harry stand at the door. Hagrid in the moleskin coat, umbrella, beard, too big for the pavement. Harry in Dudley’s clothes, blanket left behind, letter in his jacket, taped glasses, scar. Pedestrians in coats and office clothes, none looking at the pair.
- **Faces:** A woman with a pram looks through Hagrid as if he were a lamppost. Harry looks at her, confused. Hagrid looks at the door, fond.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Leaky Cauldron. Don't mind that the Muggles walk straight past it."

#### C4-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Inside, the room goes quiet. Someone whispers Harry’s name.
- **Image:** A crowded pub frozen mid-drink. Every head turns. The name is a small white speech breath near an old man’s mouth, not a long line of dialogue.
- **Setting:** The Leaky Cauldron. Low beams, a fire, copper pans, odd customers in cloaks and hats, a bar, smoke. Daylight only at the door they entered.
- **Who:** Harry small in the doorway, oversized jacket, jeans, glasses, scar suddenly visible because he pushed his hair back. Hagrid behind him, filling the door, coat. An old wizard at the bar, pointed hat, pint in hand. A barman with a rag. A witch in a bottle-green hat.
- **Faces:** The room looks at Harry with awe, not unkind. Harry looks back, overwhelmed, shoulders up. Hagrid looks proud and protective. The old man looks at the scar, whispering.
- **Dialogue:** **Old wizard:** "Bless my soul. Harry Potter. In our pub."

#### C4-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Strangers reach to shake his hand. He shrinks.
- **Image:** Many hands come into the frame from all sides, some gloved, some ink-stained. Harry is in the middle, making himself smaller. One hand is about to touch his hair near the scar.
- **Setting:** The pub crowd pressed close. Firelight. Cloaks. No table space.
- **Who:** Harry, jacket bunched in his fists, glasses, scar, looking for an exit that is not there. Hagrid’s big arm is a barrier on one side. The reaching people are mostly hands and delighted eyes: a tiny witch, a man with a monocle, a boy a little older in robes.
- **Faces:** They look at Harry as a story. Harry looks at the floor, shy and uneasy. He does not like being touched by strangers. Hagrid looks at the crowd, polite but ready to move them on.
- **Dialogue:** **Witch:** "May I shake your hand? The boy who lived." **Harry:** "Please. I don't really know what to say."

#### C4-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Hagrid taps a brick pattern with his umbrella.
- **Image:** The courtyard behind the pub. A plain brick wall. Hagrid counts bricks with the umbrella tip, tap by tap. Harry watches the wall, not the giant.
- **Setting:** A small grimy yard, bins, a cat that is only a cat, gray daylight. The wall looks like nothing.
- **Who:** Hagrid bent over, coat, umbrella pointing, tongue slightly out as he counts. Harry beside him, hands in jacket pockets, letter tucked inside, sneakers on damp stone.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the bricks, concentrating, cheerful. Harry looks at the wall, skeptical and curious. The cat looks at neither.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Three up, two across. Watch the bricks."

#### C4-P1-P5

- **Beat:** The wall folds open.
- **Image:** Bricks peel back like a puzzle, a hole widening into light and color. Harry’s hair blows forward. Hagrid does not even step back.
- **Setting:** The grimy yard on the near side. On the far side, a blaze of a street not yet fully shown: a hint of a cauldron stack, an owl, a striped awning.
- **Who:** Harry, jacket, glasses flashing, both hands lifted against the sudden light. Hagrid, umbrella lowered, beard parted in a grin. No one else.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the opening, eyes huge, fear gone for a second. Hagrid looks at Harry’s face, not at the street, because the boy’s reaction is the picture he wanted.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C4-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Diagon Alley spills out. Harry’s eyes go wide.
- **Image:** Full wide panel, the page-turn. The alley is packed and crooked and wonderful: cauldrons, broomsticks, owl cages, robe windows, a goblin, children in robes, banners. Harry and Hagrid are small at the bottom, stepping through.
- **Setting:** Diagon Alley in daytime. Cobblestones, leaning shops, a bright sky between roofs, color everywhere after the gray Muggle street.
- **Who:** Harry in Muggle castoffs, the only child dressed wrong, letter and wonder. Hagrid beside him, coat, umbrella, cake tin long gone. Shoppers in cloaks and pointed hats. An owl in a cage turning its head. A stack of pewter cauldrons as tall as Harry.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up and around, mouth open, delighted. Hagrid looks ahead, at home. A witch looks at Harry’s jeans, amused, not cruel.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "What is this place?" **Hagrid:** "Diagon Alley. Where wizards do their shopping."

### Page 2 — Gringotts

#### C4-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Marble and goblins. One goblin looks down at them.
- **Image:** A long marble counter, high. A goblin teller leans over it to see Hagrid, and still has to look down at Harry. Gold light, cool stone.
- **Setting:** Gringotts entrance hall. White marble, chandeliers, long counters, goblins in crimson uniforms, wizards queueing. Echoing and grand.
- **Who:** A goblin with long fingers, clever eyes, crimson coat, a quill. Hagrid, hatless, coat, a key on a chain in his palm. Harry beside him, jeans and jacket, craning up. Other wizards in robes wait behind.
- **Faces:** The goblin looks at Harry, sharp and unimpressed by fame. Harry looks up, polite and nervous. Hagrid looks at the goblin, respectful.
- **Dialogue:** **Goblin:** "Key." **Hagrid:** "Vault for Harry Potter. And one more, on the headmaster's order."

#### C4-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hagrid says worse things than goblins guard the deep vaults.
- **Image:** They walk a corridor of vault doors. Hagrid talks out of the side of his mouth. Harry’s eyes slide to a dark tunnel mouth.
- **Setting:** Underground already in feeling: bronze doors, goblin lamps, a drop in temperature. A warning plaque. The cart rails begin.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat, key, one hand on Harry’s shoulder. Harry, jacket zipped now, glasses, both hands holding the strap of nothing — he has no bag yet. A goblin walks ahead, crimson, lantern.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks ahead, casual about danger. Harry looks at the dark tunnel, swallowing, brave because he wants to be. The goblin does not look back.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Goblins are the least of what guards the deep vaults at Gringotts. Dragons, some of them."

#### C4-P2-P3

- **Beat:** A cart plunges on rails through the caves.
- **Image:** The cart tips down a waterfall of rails. Harry’s hair is straight up. Hagrid’s beard is a flag. Stalactites blur.
- **Setting:** Gringotts caverns. Underground lakes, rails like roller coasters, torch brackets, vast dark. Speed lines in the rock, not in the characters.
- **Who:** Harry gripping the cart rim, knuckles white, jacket flapping, glasses pressed to his face by the wind. Hagrid beside him, one hand lazy on the rail, grinning, coat snapping. The goblin drives, crouched, crimson coat, face into the wind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks forward, terrified and thrilled, mouth open. Hagrid looks at Harry, laughing. The goblin looks at the track, bored.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Does the cart have to go this fast?" **Hagrid:** "Best bit of Gringotts, this is."

#### C4-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Harry’s vault. A modest heap of coins. He has never owned anything.
- **Image:** A small vault, door open. Gold, silver, and bronze coins in a heap that is rich to Harry and ordinary to the bank. He stands at the edge, not stepping in, as if he might be yelled at.
- **Setting:** A round stone vault, torchlight, the cart waiting outside. No traps. Dust and shine.
- **Who:** Harry, Muggle clothes, glasses, hands held tight to his sides. Hagrid behind him, gentle, a leather pouch in one hand, ready to help him take some. The goblin holds the door.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the coins, stunned, eyes wet. He looks at Hagrid for permission. Hagrid looks at him, soft, nodding. The goblin looks at a pocket watch, impatient.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "This is mine? All of these coins?" **Hagrid:** "Your mum and dad left them. Galleons, Sickles, Knuts. Take a handful."

#### C4-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A deeper vault. Hagrid lifts a small grubby package into his coat.
- **Image:** A high-security vault, colder, the door thicker. In the middle, on a stone table, one small parcel wrapped in dirty paper and string. Hagrid’s hand closes around it. Harry is kept near the door.
- **Setting:** Deeper cavern light, blue and thin. The cart far behind. Goblin lantern. Scratches on the stone, as if something else used to be kept here.
- **Who:** Hagrid, suddenly serious, coat opened, package going into an inner pocket. Harry in the doorway, small, pouch of coins now in his hands, heavy. The goblin watches the package, not Harry.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the package, respectful and nervous. Harry looks at it, questions forming. The goblin looks at Hagrid’s pocket, professional.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "That's the parcel. Hogwarts business. Don't you touch it."

#### C4-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry asks what it is. Hagrid says it is Hogwarts business.
- **Image:** The cart climbs. Hagrid’s hand rests over the pocket. Harry twists in the seat to see his face. The package makes a square shape under the coat.
- **Setting:** The rails rising toward distant daylight. Wind. Gold sparks of other vaults far below.
- **Who:** Harry, hair wild, glasses, coin pouch in his lap, jacket. Hagrid, one big hand flat on his chest over the hidden parcel, the other on the cart. The goblin drives.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, curious, not pushy. Hagrid looks ahead, mouth shut, kind but closed. He will not tell. Harry accepts it with a small frown.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "What's in it?" **Hagrid:** "Can't say. Dumbledore's orders."

### Page 3 — Supplies

#### C4-P3-P1

- **Beat:** A measuring tape moves by itself.
- **Image:** Harry on a stool in a robe shop, arms out. A tape measure zips around him with no tailor holding it. Pins float.
- **Setting:** Madam Malkin’s. Mirrors, bolts of black cloth, a window onto the alley. Another boy is a pale blur in the next mirror, not clearly Draco yet.
- **Who:** Harry in his jeans and jacket, sleeves being measured, glasses, scar, trying to stand still. A witch in the background with pins in her mouth, mauve robes. The tape is the actor.
