## Chapter 9 — Flying class and the Seeker

Brooms, the theft, the dive, and a place on the team.

### Page 1 — Brooms

#### C8-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Dawn lawn. Two lines of students. Brooms on the grass.
- **Image:** A wide, cold, beautiful panel. Dew. Two rows of first years. A broom lies in front of each like a sleeping animal. The castle is pink with sunrise behind them.
- **Setting:** The Hogwarts lawn at dawn. Mist low. The Quidditch pitch is a distant oval. Birds. Madam Hooch’s whistle not yet blown.
- **Who:** Madam Hooch, short gray hair, yellow hawk eyes, dark flying cloak, whistle, boots, standing at the end of the lines. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, Draco, and the others in robes or simplified flying gear, each behind a broom. Harry’s broom is old and twiggy. Draco’s looks newer.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks along the lines, sharp. Harry looks at his broom, curious, a pull in his hands. Neville looks at his, already afraid. Draco looks at Neville, scenting weakness. Hermione looks determined.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Brooms on the grass. Stand beside them. Good morning."

#### C8-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Madam Hooch says nobody kicks off until she says.
- **Image:** Her hand is up, flat, a command. Whistle in the other. Students have their hands over their brooms, not yet touching. Neville’s fingers twitch.
- **Setting:** The dew lawn, close. Broom bristles detailed. Sunrise side-light.
- **Who:** Hooch, yellow eyes, cloak, whistle at her lips almost. Neville, sandy hair, robes, hand drifting down too early. Harry, glasses, hand correctly hovering. Draco, platinum hair, relaxed, watching Neville not Hooch.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at all of them, stern care. Neville looks at his broom, anxious to get it wrong over with. Draco looks at Neville, a smile starting. Harry looks at Hooch, listening.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Right hand over the broom, and say up. Nobody kicks off until I give the word."

#### C8-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Neville kicks off early. The broom shoots up.
- **Image:** Neville is yanked vertical. The broom stands like a pole and hauls him. His feet leave the grass. Hooch’s whistle is a second too late. Dew scatters.
- **Setting:** The lawn, the lines breaking as people look up. Sky pale.
- **Who:** Neville, robes flapping, both hands locked on the handle, sandy hair blown, face already losing the ground. His broom, wild. Hooch starting to move. Harry stepping forward and stopping because he was told not to. Draco delighted.
- **Faces:** Neville looks down, terror. Hooch looks up, alarmed, professional. Harry looks up, worried. Draco looks up, laughing.
- **Dialogue:** **Neville:** "Up!" **Madam Hooch:** "Mr Longbottom, I did not say go!"

#### C8-P1-P4

- **Beat:** He hangs on, circling, terrified.
- **Image:** From the ground, Neville is a struggling shape against the morning sky, circling because he cannot steer. One hand is slipping. The broom bucks.
- **Setting:** Open sky, a tower at the edge, the class small below. Cold air.
- **Who:** Neville, robes, white-knuckled, one shoe loose. The broom. Below, out of focus, Hooch shouting instructions he cannot use.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at his slipping hand, then at the ground, mouth open, no pride left. He looks at Harry for half a second as if Harry could catch him. Harry, tiny below, looks up, helpless.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Come down! Lean forward, Mr Longbottom, and come down this instant!"

#### C8-P1-P5

- **Beat:** He falls. A crack. His wrist is wrong.
- **Image:** Neville hits the grass. The sound is a crack drawn as a sharp white break in the panel border near his wrist. The broom clatters beside him. The Remembrall, a small glass ball, rolls from his pocket.
- **Setting:** The lawn, dew, the class in a circle now, sunrise.
- **Who:** Neville on his back, robes tangled, sandy hair, one wrist clutched, face screwed. The glass Remembrall rolling toward Draco’s foot, smoke-white inside. Hooch kneeling. Harry and Hermione close. Ron pale.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at his wrist, crying, ashamed as well as hurt. Hooch looks at the wrist, competent concern. Hermione looks at Neville, worried. Draco looks at the rolling ball, not at Neville.
- **Dialogue:** **Neville:** "My wrist. I think it's broken."

#### C8-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Hooch takes him to the castle and orders the class to stay down.
- **Image:** She levitates Neville gently, his wrist supported, and points a strict finger at the class. Her yellow eyes promise consequences. They are small on the grass with their brooms.
- **Setting:** The lawn, the castle doors in the distance where she is headed. The Remembrall has stopped near Draco.
- **Who:** Hooch, cloak, whistle, one hand wand-guiding Neville, the other pointing down: stay. Neville floating, crying, looking back at his broom. Draco’s shoe beside the Remembrall. Harry, Ron, Hermione standing still, obedient. The rest of the class frozen.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at the class, severe. Neville looks back at the ball he dropped, distressed. Draco looks at the ball, thoughtful. Harry looks at Hooch, nodding, he will stay. He means it, for about one panel.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Hospital wing for you. The rest of you, feet on the grass. One broom in the air and you are out of Hogwarts before you can sneeze."

### Page 2 — The theft

#### C8-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Draco picks up the glass ball that rolled from Neville’s pocket.
- **Image:** Draco crouches, elegant even there, and pinches the Remembrall between two fingers like something faintly dirty. Smoke swirls inside it.
- **Setting:** The dew grass, abandoned brooms, the castle swallowing Hooch and Neville. The class is an uneasy ring.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, fine robes, the Remembrall held up to the light. Crabbe and Goyle shadowing him. Harry a few steps away. Hermione with her arms folded. Ron glaring.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the ball, then at Harry, a game beginning. Harry looks at the ball, recognizing it is not Draco’s. Hermione looks at Draco, warning. Ron looks at Draco’s hand, angry.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "What's this bit of glass Longbottom dropped?"

#### C8-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione says it is a Remembrall, a gift, and to give it back.
- **Image:** Hermione steps in, chin up, books nowhere, both feet planted. She is smaller than Draco and clearer. He holds the ball just out of her reach, childish and precise.
- **Setting:** The lawn. Wind in the grass. Other students watching, unwilling to side against him.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, empty hands, robes. Draco, ball between finger and thumb, high. Harry moving closer. Ron at Harry’s shoulder.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Draco, stern, in the right. Draco looks at her, amused, not threatened. He looks at Harry to see if this is working. Harry looks at the ball, deciding.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's a Remembrall. His grandmother sent it. Give it back, Malfoy."

#### C8-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Draco mounts. He tells Harry to try to take it.
- **Image:** Draco is astride the broom, already a foot off the ground, the Remembrall dangling. He crooks a finger. Harry’s broom is at his feet.
- **Setting:** Open lawn, sky, the forbidden air. The class steps back.
- **Who:** Draco hovering, platinum hair barely moved, expensive posture, ball in one hand, broom in the other. Harry on the ground, glasses, robes, his own broom by his shoe. Hermione reaching for Harry’s sleeve. Ron looking at the sky, torn.
- **Faces:** Draco looks down at Harry, baiting, smile sharp. Harry looks up, the decision already in his shoulders. Hermione looks at Harry, alarmed. She looks at the castle, where Hooch went.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Come and take it, Potter. If you can fly."

#### C8-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Harry mounts. Hermione says he will be expelled.
- **Image:** Harry swings a leg over. Hermione’s hand catches empty air. The broom stirs under him before he kicks off, eager.
- **Setting:** Grass, dawn fully up now, long shadows.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, both hands on the broom, sneakers hooked. Hermione, hair blown, one hand out, face urgent. Ron stepping back to give him room, loyal and scared. Draco above, waiting.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Harry, pleading, the word expelled serious to her. Harry looks at the ball, not at her. His mouth is sorry and his eyes are already gone. Ron looks at Harry, a nod. Draco looks delighted.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Harry, no. Madam Hooch will have you expelled."

#### C8-P2-P5

- **Beat:** He kicks off. The ground drops. This feels right.
- **Image:** Harry rises. The lawn falls away cleanly. His body knows the broom. Hair and robes stream. His face changes from stubborn to astonished joy.
- **Setting:** Air. The castle tilting. The class shrinking. Clouds.
- **Who:** Harry alone in the sky, broom, glasses, scar, hands easy now, not tight. Tiny Draco ahead with the glint of the ball. Tiny Hermione below with her hands on her head.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his own hands on the broom, then forward, a wild gladness. He looks like someone who has been in a cupboard and has just found the sky. No fear.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C8-P2-P6

- **Beat:** He levels with Draco above the towers.
- **Image:** Two boys and the castle towers below them. Wind. Draco is no longer higher. Harry is beside him, steady. The ball is between them in Draco’s fist.
- **Setting:** High above Hogwarts. Towers, battlements, a bird below them, the lake a dark coin. Morning gold.
- **Who:** Harry, robes snapping, glasses, messy hair worse and better, broom an extension of his arms. Draco, platinum hair finally disturbed, fine robes, the Remembrall clenched, his hover less sure.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Draco, calm, the joy banked into focus. Draco looks at Harry, the smile slipping, surprised to be matched. He looks at the ball, then at Harry’s hands.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Not bad, Potter. For a boy brought up by Muggles."

### Page 3 — The catch

#### C8-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Draco throws the Remembrall. It falls toward stone.
- **Image:** Draco’s hand open, spiteful. The glass ball drops past Harry’s knee toward a courtyard far below. It is small and getting smaller. Harry’s head snaps down.
- **Setting:** Above a stone courtyard, flagstones rushing away in perspective. Towers on either side.
- **Who:** Draco, arm extended from the throw, sneer returning. Harry, body already tipping, eyes tracking the ball. The Remembrall, clear glass, white smoke inside, falling.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, expecting him to freeze. Harry looks only at the ball, the world narrowed. His mouth is set. Fear is there and it is not in charge.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Fetch, Potter."

#### C8-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Harry dives. Wind tears at his glasses.
- **Image:** A vertical panel feeling even inside the grid: Harry arrowed down, broom screaming, the ball a target below. His glasses slide; he jams them back with one finger without losing the line.
- **Setting:** Stone rushing up. A tower window. A gargoyle. The ball.
- **Who:** Harry, robes plastered by speed, hair straight back, glasses, one hand on them, the other arm forward. The broom. No one else can keep up. Draco is a speck above.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ball, eyes watering from the wind, intent, not heroic for an audience. Concentration.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C8-P3-P3

- **Beat:** The courtyard rushes up. Students scream.
- **Image:** From the ground, students who have run to the courtyard cover their mouths. Harry is a blur coming down. The ball is inches from the flagstones. It looks too late.
- **Setting:** A courtyard of gray stone, morning, a few older students, Hermione and Ron arriving at a run, a teacher’s distant shout.
- **Who:** Hermione, hands over her mouth, bushy hair. Ron, white under his freckles, neck craned. Other students scattering. Harry large at the top of the panel, hand outstretched. The Remembrall at the bottom, about to break.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks up, terrified. Ron looks up, unable to blink. A student looks away. Harry’s face, distant, is all focus.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Harry!" **Ron:** "Pull up! Pull up!"

#### C8-P3-P4

- **Beat:** His hand closes on the ball inches from the flagstones.
- **Image:** The catch, inches above stone. Fingers around glass. The broom’s bristles skim a flagstone and spark. His knee almost hits. He has it.
- **Setting:** Extreme low angle on the courtyard floor. The ball and the hand and the stone. Dust.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, robe cuff, the Remembrall intact, white smoke swirling as if relieved. A slice of his face, glasses, scar, teeth gritted. The broom.
- **Faces:** He looks at the ball in his fist, shock that it is there. Not triumph yet. Disbelief and grip.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C8-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He pulls up. Grass and dust flatten. He is shaking and grinning.
- **Image:** He levels out so low that dust and a discarded leaf blast sideways. Then he is upright on the broom, hovering, shaking. The grin arrives late, after the fear.
- **Setting:** The courtyard. Students upright again. Sun. The tower he fell from high above, Draco a tiny angry shape.
- **Who:** Harry hovering, robes, hair wrecked, glasses, the Remembrall raised a little, his free hand trembling on the broom. Ron whooping, running in. Hermione furious and relieved, hands still near her face.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, a shaky grin. He looks at his own hand, laughing once. Ron looks at him, ecstatic. Hermione looks at him, angry because she was frightened. Draco, far, looks down, smile gone.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "You caught it!" **Harry:** "I've got Neville's Remembrall."

#### C8-P3-P6

- **Beat:** McGonagall, from the steps, icy: Potter, with her.
- **Image:** She stands on the castle steps, emerald sharp against the stone, finger pointing at him. The courtyard goes quiet. Harry’s grin dies. He drifts down.
- **Setting:** The entrance steps. Morning. The crowd parted. Hooch is not back yet. This is worse: it is McGonagall.
- **Who:** McGonagall, bun, square spectacles, emerald robes, hat, one finger extended, the other hand behind her back. Harry landing, Remembrall in his fist, dusty, small. Ron and Hermione frozen. Draco landing apart, suddenly innocent, empty hands.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Harry, icy, unreadable. Harry looks at her, the sky-joy gone, waiting to be finished. He holds the ball out a little, as if it explains him. She does not look at the ball.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Potter. With me. Now."

### Page 4 — Seeker

#### C8-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Harry follows, sure he is finished.
- **Image:** A corridor. Her heels are precise. He trots behind, the Remembrall still in his hand because he does not know where to put it. Portraits peer.
- **Setting:** Inside the castle, a long gallery, morning light, armor, a red banner. Echo.
- **Who:** McGonagall ahead, emerald, not looking back. Harry behind, dusty robes, glasses, hair destroyed by the dive, ball in one hand, the other reaching to fix nothing. A portrait of a knight leans out to stare.
- **Faces:** McGonagall’s back is straight, no comfort. Harry looks at her back, resigned, the cupboard-feeling returning. The portrait looks at him, curious.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Professor, it was Neville's Remembrall. I was only..." **McGonagall:** "Silence, Potter."

#### C8-P4-P2

- **Beat:** She opens a door. Oliver Wood looks up from a Quidditch diagram.
- **Image:** A small room, a chalkboard of Quidditch arrows, broom in the corner. Wood looks up, older, intense. McGonagall is in the door with a first year, which is not what he expected.
- **Setting:** A team room. Pads, a Quaffle, red and gold, morning through a high window. The diagram is a mess of brilliant tactics.
- **Who:** Oliver Wood, brown hair, Gryffindor practice robes, a leather arm guard, chalk in hand. McGonagall, emerald, ushering. Harry, dusty, the glass ball absurd in a strategy room, glasses, scar.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at Harry, puzzled, measuring. McGonagall looks at Wood, the ice becoming purpose. Harry looks at the diagram, lost, still expecting detention.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Mr Wood. A moment of your tactics." **Wood:** "Professor McGonagall?"

#### C8-P4-P3

- **Beat:** She says she has found him a Seeker.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s hand is on Harry’s shoulder, the first gentle touch from her. She is almost smiling. Wood’s chalk stops. Harry blinks.
- **Setting:** The team room. The diagram behind them like a future.
- **Who:** McGonagall, spectacles, emerald, hand on Harry’s dusty shoulder. Harry, small, Remembrall, robes. Wood, arm guard, chalk, leaning in.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Wood, proud of her find, a rare warmth. She looks at Harry, brisk approval. Wood looks at Harry’s hands, then his size, interest replacing doubt. Harry looks at her, the word Seeker not landed yet. His mouth is open.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "I have found you a Seeker." **Wood:** "A first year?"

#### C8-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Wood measures Harry’s size, then his hands, then grins.
- **Image:** Wood crouches to Harry’s height, takes in the skinny shoulders, then turns Harry’s free hand over, the one that caught the ball. The grin is a captain seeing a season.
- **Setting:** The team room, Quaffle on a bench, red light from a banner.
- **Who:** Wood, practice robes, arm guard, both hands around the idea of Harry. Harry, glasses, scar, the Remembrall in the other fist, standing still for inspection. McGonagall by the door, arms folded, satisfied.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at the catching hand, then at Harry’s face, a grin breaking. Harry looks at Wood, hope arriving carefully. McGonagall looks at them, the icy panel from the steps rewritten.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Small. Light on a broom. And look at that catching hand. You'll do, Potter."

#### C8-P4-P5

- **Beat:** On the walk, Wood explains the Snitch in three quick sketches.
- **Image:** They walk a cloister. Wood draws in the air with his finger: a tiny gold ball, hoops, Harry as a dot. Harry’s eyes track the invisible sketches and only half keep up. He still holds Neville’s ball.
- **Setting:** An open cloister, lawns through the arches, late morning, students passing and staring at a first year with the Quidditch captain.
- **Who:** Wood, tall, brown hair, gesturing a whole sport. Harry trotting beside, dusty robes, glasses, Remembrall, nodding too fast. A few older students noticing.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at the air where his diagram is, passionate. Harry looks at Wood’s hands, excited and behind. He looks like a boy who has been chosen and is afraid to drop it.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "The Golden Snitch is worth one hundred and fifty points. Catch it, and the match is nearly always yours. A Seeker lives for that glint, and nothing else."

#### C8-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Ron and Hermione ambush him. He holds up the Remembrall and says he is on the team.
- **Image:** Page-turn. Around a corner, Ron and Hermione are waiting, braced for expulsion news. Harry holds up the glass ball like a trophy and his face is the news. Ron yells. Hermione’s anger collapses into a laugh she did not schedule.
- **Setting:** A sunny corridor corner. Armor. A window. The end of the morning.
- **Who:** Harry, dusty, grinning, Remembrall raised, glasses, scar, robes. Ron, red hair, freckles, both fists up, shouting. Hermione, books, hair, one hand over her smile, the other hitting Harry’s arm lightly for scaring her.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, proud and disbelieving, the sky still in his eyes. Ron looks at him, joy. Hermione looks at him, fond and exasperated, the expulsion fear leaving. The ball looks tiny and important between them.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I'm on the Gryffindor Quidditch team." **Ron:** "You're what?" **Hermione:** "You were supposed to be expelled."

## Chapter 10 — The Midnight Duel

A trap, a chase, Fluffy, and the memory of a package.

### Page 1 — The challenge

#### C9-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Draco stops Harry in the entrance hall, Crabbe and Goyle behind him.
- **Image:** The entrance hall is busy with afternoon light. Draco steps into Harry’s path so neatly it looks polite. The two big boys close the gap behind.
- **Setting:** Hogwarts entrance hall. Stone, the front doors open to sun, students, a staircase, the ceiling high.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes, green lining, hands behind his back. Crabbe and Goyle, bulky, shadows. Harry, clean robes again, glasses, bag, scar. Ron beside him. Hermione a step back, already suspicious.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, pleasant and false. Harry looks at him, guarded. Ron looks at Crabbe and Goyle, measuring the exit. Hermione looks at Draco’s smile, not buying it.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Potter. A word. Without the Weasley, if you can manage it."

#### C9-P1-P2

- **Beat:** A wizard’s duel. Midnight. The trophy room. No teachers.
- **Image:** Draco leans in, voice low, so the hall cannot hear. His finger taps an imaginary watch. Harry’s bag strap tightens in his fist.
- **Setting:** A quieter side of the hall, under a suit of armor. Sun and dust.
- **Who:** Draco close, sleek, the challenge delivered like a gift. Harry, chin slightly down, listening. Ron leaning in, red hair, already tempted. The armor.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, eyes bright with the trick, smile small. Harry looks at Draco’s mouth, weighing pride against sense. Ron looks at Harry, eager, the decision tilting the wrong way.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "A wizard's duel. Midnight. The trophy room. Wands only. No teachers, unless you're frightened."

#### C9-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says it is a trap and against a stack of rules.
- **Image:** She inserts herself, a book pressed to her chest like a shield of regulations. She is listing rules on her fingers. Draco is already walking away, job done.
- **Setting:** The hall. Draco’s back receding with Crabbe and Goyle. A clock.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, book, three fingers up and more coming. Harry and Ron, bags, being lectured. Draco from behind, platinum hair, unhurried.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the boys, urgent, right, and annoyed she has to be. Harry looks at Draco’s back, pride prickling. Ron looks at Hermione, defensive. Draco does not look back; his shoulders look pleased.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's a trap. And it breaks about fifty school rules, starting with curfew."

#### C9-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Harry looks at Ron. Ron says they have to go.
- **Image:** A two-shot in a window alcove. The afternoon is gold. Ron’s mind is made. Harry’s is making itself. Hermione is a frustrated blur walking ahead.
- **Setting:** A corridor alcove, suits of armor, a view of the lawn where Harry flew.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bag held in both hands. Ron, freckles, maroon sweater cuffs, hands open, convincing. Hermione ahead, not in the decision.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, loyal and reckless, a grin. Harry looks at Ron, the flying-courage still in him, nodding once. He looks worried and he nods anyway.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "We have to go. If you don't, he'll tell the whole school you ran." **Harry:** "All right. Midnight."

#### C9-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Night. Two boys slip out of the Gryffindor corridor.
- **Image:** A portrait hole swings. Harry and Ron creep out in pajamas and dressing gowns, wands out, ridiculous and brave. The common room fire behind them is the last warm light.
- **Setting:** Night castle. The Gryffindor entrance, a painting swung open, stone corridor, moonlight through a high window. Torches low.
- **Who:** Harry in oversized gray pajamas, glasses, wand, bare feet in slippers, hair worse from bed. Ron in a maroon pajama top that was a brother’s, wand, freckles, red hair. The Fat Lady’s portrait, annoyed, in a pink dress, swinging shut.
- **Faces:** Harry looks down the corridor, alert. Ron looks at Harry, a nervous grin. The Fat Lady looks at them, disapproving, mouth pursed.
- **Dialogue:** **Fat Lady:** "Out at this hour? I shall pretend to be asleep. I am a very poor pretender."

#### C9-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Hermione is already waiting, furious and whispering.
- **Image:** She steps from behind a suit of armor, fully dressed, arms folded, whisper-shouting. They jump. Her hair is a cloud of indignation.
- **Setting:** The moonlit corridor. Armor. A tapestry. Far off, a torch.
- **Who:** Hermione in her day robes and perfect tie even at midnight, wand in hand, books not with her for once. Harry and Ron in pajamas, wands up then lowered, guilty.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at them, furious, whispering, frightened under the fury. Harry looks at her, caught. Ron looks at her, betrayed by her competence. She looks back the way they came, wanting them to return, and they do not.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "You perfect idiots. Turn round before Filch catches you."

### Page 2 — The trap

#### C9-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Neville, locked out in his nightshirt, begs to come.
- **Image:** Neville stands in the corridor in a pale nightshirt, toad in one hand, the portrait hole shut behind him. He looks cold and abandoned. He grabs Ron’s sleeve.
- **Setting:** Outside the common room. Night. The Fat Lady’s portrait is turned away, refusing to open.
- **Who:** Neville, sandy hair, long nightshirt, slippers missing one, Trevor the toad in his palm. Ron in maroon pajamas. Harry in gray. Hermione, robes, exasperated anew.
- **Faces:** Neville looks at them, pleading, ashamed, he forgot the password. Ron looks at the toad, then at Neville, groaning a yes. Hermione looks at Neville, sympathy winning over rules. Harry looks down the corridor, time running out.
- **Dialogue:** **Neville:** "The Fat Lady won't let me in. I forgot the password. Let me come with you. Please."

#### C9-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The trophy room is empty. Draco is not there.
- **Image:** Glass cases of cups and shields. Moonlight. No Draco. Their four reflections look back at them, small and foolish.
- **Setting:** The trophy room. Silver and gold catching moonlight. A polished floor. Silence. Banners in cases.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville, pajamas and one set of robes, wands lowered. Trevor on Neville’s shoulder. No Slytherins. Their reflections.
- **Faces:** Harry looks around, suspicion arriving. Ron looks at the door they entered, uneasy. Hermione looks at Harry with an I-told-you-so she does not enjoy. Neville looks at a big cup, briefly distracted, then scared again.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Malfoy's not here." **Harry:** "Then it was a trick."

#### C9-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Filch’s lamp turns the corner. His cat’s eyes gleam.
- **Image:** A sick yellow lamp. Filch’s stooped shape. At his ankles, Mrs. Norris, a skinny dust-colored cat, eyes like lamps of her own. The children are at the trophy-room door, caught in the edge of the light.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the trophy room. Night. Stone. The lamp makes huge shadows.
- **Who:** Argus Filch, stringy gray hair, brown shabby coat, a bunch of keys, the lamp, a furious stoop. Mrs. Norris, hairless-looking cat, tail stiff. The four students just inside the light’s reach.
- **Faces:** Filch looks down the corridor, hunting, a grim joy. Mrs. Norris looks straight at the door, she knows. Harry looks at the cat, alarm. Hermione looks at a side passage, calculating.
- **Dialogue:** **Filch:** "Who's sneaking? Mrs Norris can smell students. Come out."

#### C9-P2-P4

- **Beat:** They run. Hermione hisses that she told them.
- **Image:** Four sets of feet running. Hermione is half-turned, whisper-shouting, still faster than Neville. The lamp light chases their heels.
- **Setting:** A narrow corridor, suits of armor they bang into, a tapestry they fling aside. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione in front, robes flying, wand lit faintly. Harry pulling Neville. Ron behind, red hair, looking back at the lamp. Neville, nightshirt, toad clutched to his chest, gasping.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks back at the boys, angry and scared. Harry looks at Neville, making sure he is there. Ron looks back at Filch’s light, pale. Neville looks at the floor, trying not to fall.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I told you. I told you it was a trap."

#### C9-P2-P5

- **Beat:** A door. They pile through.
- **Image:** Harry hits a door and it opens. They tumble in a knot of pajamas, robes, and toad. The door is tall and they were not looking at the sign of the corridor.
- **Setting:** A threshold. Behind them the corridor and Filch’s distant lamp. Ahead, darkness and a smell of dog, not seen yet.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand on the door, gray pajamas. Ron falling past him. Hermione grabbing Neville’s nightshirt. Neville hugging Trevor. All four crossing at once.
- **Faces:** Harry looks into the dark room, not yet afraid of the right thing. Hermione looks back at the corridor, relieved for one second. Neville’s eyes are shut.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "In here. Quiet."

#### C9-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Filch passes. They breathe. A growl comes from behind them.
- **Image:** They are pressed to the inside of the door, listening. Filch’s lamp light slides under the door and moves on. Their shoulders drop. Then all four faces change, because the growl is in the room with them, low and triple.
- **Setting:** Pitch dark inside. A line of lamplight under the door, then gone. Stone floor. The growl vibrates dust.
- **Who:** The four, backs to the door, faces turning over their shoulders into the black. Harry, glasses glinting. Ron, freckles gone pale. Hermione, wand raised, the light not on yet. Neville, toad under his chin.
- **Faces:** Relief collapses. Harry looks into the dark, eyes wide. Hermione looks past him, dread. Ron looks at Harry. Neville looks like he might faint before he sees anything.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "He's gone past." **Harry:** "Then what was that growl?"

### Page 3 — Fluffy

#### C9-P3-P1

- **Beat:** A dog the size of the room. Three heads.
- **Image:** Hermione’s wand-light flares. Fluffy fills the room. Three huge heads, three sets of teeth, one massive body, paws the size of doors. The children are a strip of pajamas at the bottom.
- **Setting:** The forbidden third-floor corridor room. Bare stone, a high ceiling that is still not high enough, chains or none — he is simply there. Wand-light and eye-shine.
- **Who:** Fluffy, a monstrous three-headed dog, shaggy, brown-black, drool, three collars maybe, eyes catching the wand. Harry, Ron, Hermione, Neville tiny in front of the door. Trevor leaping from Neville’s hands.
- **Faces:** All three dog heads look at the children, lips back, a growl in stereo. Harry looks up, awe and terror. Hermione looks at the paws, thinking even now. Ron looks at the middle teeth. Neville’s face is already slack.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Lumos." **Ron:** "That's not a dog. That's three dogs."

#### C9-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Three different snarls. Neville faints.
- **Image:** Each head snarls on its own: left furious, middle curious-cruel, right barking. Neville’s eyes roll and he goes down in a pale heap. Trevor lands on his chest.
- **Setting:** The stone room, wand-light swinging as Hermione jolts.
- **Who:** Fluffy’s three heads, detailed, wet noses, huge teeth. Neville on the floor, nightshirt, sandy hair, peaceful in the faint. Trevor the toad, indignant. Harry and Ron grabbing Neville’s arms. Hermione holding the light.
- **Faces:** The dog heads look at the fallen boy and the standing ones, distracted between targets. Harry looks at Neville, urgent. Ron looks at the left head, which looked at him. Hermione looks at the floor under the paws.
- **Dialogue:** **Neville:** "I don't feel well."

#### C9-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry sees the paws planted on a trapdoor.
- **Image:** Between the huge paws, a wooden trapdoor with an iron ring. Harry’s eyeline is a clear arrow to it. The dog is not loose in a room; it is a lid.
- **Setting:** The floor, highlighted. Stone, scratches from claws, the trapdoor centered. Wand-light.
- **Who:** Fluffy’s paws and lower legs, shaggy. The trapdoor. Harry crouching as he pulls Neville, glasses, scar, gray pajamas, eyes up from the door to the heads. Hermione seeing it a half-second later.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the trapdoor, understanding, then back at the heads, fear sharpened by a question. Hermione looks at the same spot, mind racing. The middle head looks down at them, drool falling near the ring.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Look under the paws. A trapdoor. It's standing guard on something."

#### C9-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They drag Neville. A head snaps. Ron’s sleeve tears.
- **Image:** A head strikes. Ron’s maroon sleeve comes away in a strip. They are hauling Neville’s limp body toward the door. Harry’s slipper is lost.
- **Setting:** The room, chaos, wand-light strobing. The door behind them, not open yet.
- **Who:** Ron, sleeve torn, freckles, red hair, yanking Neville’s ankle, face twisted. A dog head, teeth just missing skin, cloth in its teeth. Harry under Neville’s arms, glasses crooked. Hermione at the door, hand on the handle, light in the other hand.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his torn sleeve, then at the teeth, shocked. Harry looks at the door, pulling. The dog head looks cheated, snarling. Hermione looks at them, come on, come on.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "My sleeve! Pull, Harry, pull!"

#### C9-P3-P5

- **Beat:** The door slams. Wood booms as a head hits it.
- **Image:** The door shut. A huge impact bulges the wood. Dust jumps from the frame. They are on the safe side in a pile. The boom is the whole panel.
- **Setting:** The third-floor corridor, night, outside the door. Moonlight. Their pile of bodies.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, and fainted Neville in a heap against the door. Trevor hopping clear. A bulge in the door at head height, then another. Harry’s hand still on the latch.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the bulging wood, panting, alive. Hermione looks at the latch, making sure it holds. Ron looks at Neville, checking him. Neville’s face is slack, peaceful, missing everything.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C9-P3-P6

- **Beat:** They listen until the growling fades.
- **Image:** Quiet panel after the noise. They sit on the corridor floor. The door is still. Neville stirs. No one speaks yet. Moonlight on the torn sleeve and the lost slipper in Harry’s hand.
- **Setting:** The empty forbidden corridor. High windows. Night. The castle asleep and not asleep.
- **Who:** The four, pajamas and robes, sitting. Harry, one slipper, glasses, scar, the other slipper in his hand. Ron, torn sleeve, red hair stuck to his forehead. Hermione, robes, wand dark now. Neville waking, toad on his knee.
- **Faces:** They look at the door, then at each other, shaky. Harry looks at Ron, a breath of a laugh that is mostly terror. Hermione looks at Harry, not ready to scold. Neville looks at them, confused, about to be told.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Has it stopped?" **Harry:** "I think so. Don't open it again."

### Page 4 — The package

#### C9-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione says the dog is guarding the trapdoor.
- **Image:** They huddle in an alcove on the way back. Hermione draws the trapdoor in the dust with her finger, and a paw print over it. The others lean in.
- **Setting:** A side alcove, tapestry, moonlight, far from the dog. The castle quiet.
- **Who:** Hermione crouching, robes, finger in the dust, hair falling forward. Harry, Ron, Neville around her, pajamas, Neville with Trevor. The dust diagram.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the drawing, certain, voice low. She looks up at them to make sure they see it. Harry looks at the paw in the dust, nodding. Ron looks at his torn sleeve, believing. Neville looks lost but listening.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "That dog isn't guarding the room. It's guarding the trapdoor."

#### C9-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Harry remembers the package from Gringotts.
- **Image:** Harry’s face changes. The dusty diagram is forgotten. His eyes go elsewhere. One hand touches his own chest where a pocket would be, the memory of Hagrid’s coat.
- **Setting:** The alcove. Moonlight on his glasses.
- **Who:** Harry, gray pajamas, glasses, scar, sitting back on his heels. The others watch him. No package is here; he is holding the memory.
- **Faces:** Harry looks through them, remembering, uneasy. Ron looks at him, waiting. Hermione looks at him, ready to connect it. Neville looks at Trevor, sleepy.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hagrid took a parcel out of a high-security vault at Gringotts. He wouldn't tell me what it was."

#### C9-P4-P3

- **Beat:** A small flash of Hagrid hiding the parcel in his coat.
- **Image:** A memory panel with softer edges. The Gringotts vault. Hagrid’s big hand puts a grubby wrapped package into his moleskin coat. Harry is small in the vault doorway, watching, as he was.
- **Setting:** The deep vault, blue lantern light, stone table, the cart behind. It is clearly a memory: slightly faded, a ragged panel edge.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat open, beard, the dirty paper parcel. Harry then, in Muggle jacket and new understanding, coin pouch in his hands. The goblin’s lantern.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the package, serious, not at Harry. Memory-Harry looks at the package, curious. Present feeling: this is the thing under the dog.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C9-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Ron says it is not their problem.
- **Image:** Back in the alcove. Ron stands, wrapping his torn sleeve around his arm, ready to be sensible for once. He points back toward the common room, the safe direction.
- **Setting:** The corridor, moonlight, the way home.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon pajamas, torn sleeve, freckles, red hair, one hand pointing home, the other holding the rip. Harry still on the floor, thinking. Hermione arms folded. Neville swaying with sleep, toad in both hands.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, pleading to be ordinary. He is scared and covering it with practicality. Harry looks at the floor, not convinced. Hermione looks at Ron, wanting to agree and not quite.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Not our problem. We nearly got eaten. Bed."

#### C9-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione says it became their problem when they saw it.
- **Image:** She offers Harry a hand up. Her face is set. The dust on her finger is still there. She is not thrilled. She is accurate.
- **Setting:** The alcove, the moon moving, time passing, they must go before Filch returns.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, hair, dusty finger, other hand out to Harry. Harry taking it, pajamas, glasses. Ron sighing, shoulders down. Neville already walking, half asleep.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Harry, grave, a shared responsibility. Harry looks at her, agreeing without a speech. Ron looks at both of them, loyal and doomed. Neville looks at the dark, he wants his bed.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It became our problem the moment we saw it."

#### C9-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Harry looks up toward the third floor and does not answer.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They walk away small. Harry is last. He looks back and up, toward a ceiling and a floor that hide the dog. His face is lit by a window. He does not say he will leave it alone, because he will not.
- **Setting:** A long night corridor, windows, the castle huge around four children. The third-floor direction is a dark arch above and behind.
- **Who:** Hermione leading, Neville with Trevor, Ron with the torn sleeve, Harry last in gray pajamas, one slipper, glasses, scar. No adults. The castle.
- **Faces:** The others look ahead, exhausted. Harry looks back and up, thoughtful, the package and the trapdoor in his eyes. His mouth is closed. The question is staying with him.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.
---

## Chapter 11 — Hallowe’en

An insult, a feast, a troll, a lie, and three friends.

### Page 1 — The insult

#### C10-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Draco mimics Harry’s dive in the courtyard and falls on purpose. People laugh.
- **Image:** Draco flings himself backward in a fake swoon, one hand on his forehead, the other clutching an imaginary ball. Crabbe and Goyle bellow. A circle of students laughs because it is easier than not.
- **Setting:** A castle courtyard, daytime, between lessons. Cloisters, sun, a few brooms of older students far off on the pitch. Stone and laughter.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, expensive robes, performing on the flagstones, not actually hurt. Crabbe and Goyle, bulky, laughing with their whole bodies. A ring of students in house robes. Harry at the edge, bag in hand, glasses, scar, stopped mid-step. Ron beside him, ears going red.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry while he “falls,” making sure the target sees. The crowd looks at Draco, laughing. Harry looks at the performance, tired, not giving Draco a flinch. Ron looks at the crowd, angry on Harry’s behalf.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Look at me, I'm Potter, diving for glory and missing the floor!"

#### C10-P1-P2

- **Beat:** In the common room, Ron says Hermione is a nightmare and has no friends.
- **Image:** The Gryffindor common room is warm and red. Ron is sprawled in an armchair, talking too loudly, a hand waving at the idea of Hermione. He thinks he is being funny. He is not looking behind the chair.
- **Setting:** Evening. Fire, squashy chairs, red hangings, students doing homework, a noticeboard. Halloween is tomorrow; a paper bat hangs crooked.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, red hair, one leg over the chair arm, a book unopened. Harry on a stool nearby, quill, half-listening, glasses. Other students in the background, some smirking.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, expecting agreement, careless. Harry looks at his homework, uncomfortable, not stopping him. That discomfort matters.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "She's a nightmare. No friends, and she likes it that way."

#### C10-P1-P3

- **Beat:** She is on the other side of the chair. She heard every word.
- **Image:** The chair back splits the panel. Ron’s laughing profile on one side. Hermione’s face on the other, close, the words still hitting. A book is crushed to her chest.
- **Setting:** The same firelight. The common room noise drops out of the picture. Just the chair, the two faces.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair, perfect tie, robes, the book hugged too hard, knuckles white. Ron, only the side of his face, freckles, mouth still shaped like a joke. Harry’s hand enters, too late, reaching as if he could pull the words back.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the chair back, not at Ron, eyes bright with held tears, mouth small and furious. Ron has not seen her yet. His face is still amused, which makes it worse.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C10-P1-P4

- **Beat:** She runs. Ron looks sick the moment she is gone.
- **Image:** Hermione is a blur through the portrait hole, book clutched, hair bouncing. Ron is half out of the chair, the joke dead on his face. The Fat Lady leans away to let Hermione pass.
- **Setting:** The common room entrance. Evening. Students turning to look. The fire pops.
- **Who:** Hermione from behind, robes, shoulders tight, not looking back. Ron standing, maroon sweater, one hand out, useless. Harry standing too, quill dropped. The Fat Lady in pink, sympathetic and nosy.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the swinging portrait, sick, guilty, ears red for a new reason. Harry looks at Ron, not scolding, just sorry. The Fat Lady looks after Hermione, mouth soft.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Hermione, I didn't mean for you to..."

#### C10-P1-P5

- **Beat:** The Halloween feast. Pumpkins, candles. Harry is quiet.
- **Image:** The Great Hall in orange and gold. Pumpkins the size of desks. Floating candles. Plates full. Harry is not eating. Ron keeps glancing at the door.
- **Setting:** Halloween night in the Great Hall. Bats, pumpkins, the four tables, the staff table, warmth and noise. A place for Hermione that is empty.
- **Who:** Harry, dress robes not required — school robes, glasses, scar, a fork idle. Ron, red hair, freckles, miserable under the feast. Neville eating happily, Trevor nowhere. Draco at the green table, fine, watching the staff. Dumbledore at the center, beard, purple. Snape black. Quirrell’s purple turban.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his plate, guilty. Ron looks at the empty stretch of bench, sick. Neville looks at a pie, content. Draco looks at Quirrell for a second, uninterested, then at the food.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C10-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry keeps looking at the door she did not come through.
- **Image:** Harry turned on the bench, looking back at the great doors. Students pass with plates. The doors stay shut. His face is the quiet center of a loud feast.
- **Setting:** The hall behind him, soft. The doors tall, iron-bound, candlelight not quite reaching them.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, one hand on the bench back. Ron beside him, also turned, less sure he has the right to look. A prefect walking by with a pumpkin jug.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the doors, worried, the feast forgotten. Ron looks at the doors, guilty. He looks at Harry, wanting to go and not saying it yet.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "She still isn't here."

### Page 2 — The announcement

#### C10-P2-P1

- **Beat:** Quirrell crashes in, turban askew, and shouts that a troll is in the dungeon.
- **Image:** The great doors bang wide. Quirrell staggers in, turban slipping, pale, both arms up. The feast noise breaks. A pumpkin rolls off a table.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall, Halloween, suddenly wrong. Candles shiver. The staff stand.
- **Who:** Quirrell, purple robes, large purple turban askew so a pale scalp shows at the edge, stammer visible in his hands. Dumbledore rising, silver beard, half-moon spectacles. McGonagall standing, emerald. Snape standing, black, eyes sharp. Students twisted around. Harry and Ron on their feet.
- **Faces:** Quirrell looks at Dumbledore, terrified, or performing terror. His eyes are wide, mouth stumbling on the warning. Dumbledore looks at him, alert, the twinkle gone. Harry looks at Quirrell, startled. Snape looks at Quirrell, narrow, suspicious already.
- **Dialogue:** **Quirrell:** "T-troll! Troll in the dungeons! Thought you ought to know."

#### C10-P2-P2

- **Beat:** He faints. The hall panics.
- **Image:** Quirrell drops in a heap of purple. The hall becomes a surge. Benches scrape. A first year cries. Food is abandoned. The fainted man is almost stepped over.
- **Setting:** The hall in panic. Pumpkins, spilled juice, bats overhead, candles swinging.
- **Who:** Quirrell on the floor, turban still on, eyes shut, arms spread. Students standing on benches. Draco calm and annoyed, not scared, Crabbe and Goyle bumping people. Harry and Ron braced against the flow. Hermione is not here.
- **Faces:** Quirrell’s face is slack, a faint that looks real. A small student looks at him, screaming. Draco looks at the chaos, superior. Harry looks over the crowd toward the doors, thinking of the bathroom, not the troll.
- **Dialogue:** **Student:** "A mountain troll? Inside Hogwarts?"

#### C10-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Dumbledore’s voice cuts through. Prefects lead the houses out.
- **Image:** Dumbledore is standing on the staff table, somehow, or simply tall enough. His arms are wide and his voice is a line of calm across the panic. Prefects raise their wands like ushers. Lines start to form.
- **Setting:** The hall, mess and order fighting. House colors gathering.
- **Who:** Dumbledore, purple star robes, beard, spectacles, commanding and kind. Percy Weasley, red hair, prefect badge, organizing Gryffindor, serious. McGonagall directing. Snape already moving toward a side door, black robes, a purpose. Harry and Ron in the Gryffindor line, looking at each other, not at Percy.
- **Faces:** Dumbledore looks over the children, steady, protecting. Percy looks at his list of duties, tense. Harry looks at Ron, a decision. Ron looks back, agreeing before the words. Snape looks toward the dungeons, grim.
- **Dialogue:** **Dumbledore:** "Silence! Prefects, lead your houses to the dormitories at once. Teachers, with me."

#### C10-P2-P4

- **Beat:** In the crush, Harry grabs Ron. Hermione does not know.
- **Image:** The line surges toward the doors. Harry’s hand locks on Ron’s sleeve. He talks close, urgent. They are already drifting out of the line.
- **Setting:** The crowded doorway. Robes, elbows, prefects shouting colors. Halloween decorations underfoot.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, robes, one hand on Ron, the other pointing back into the castle, not toward the dormitory. Ron, red hair, freckles, nodding, scared and in. Percy ahead, not seeing them peel away. Hermione absent.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, urgent, the bathroom in his mind. Ron looks at Harry, guilty courage. He looks at the dormitory direction and rejects it. A younger student looks at them, confused, and is swept on.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hermione doesn't know. She's still in the bathroom. She never heard." **Ron:** "Then we go and get her."

#### C10-P2-P5

- **Beat:** They peel away toward the bathrooms.
- **Image:** The house lines go left. Two boys go right, small against the corridor. A prefect’s back is turned. Their running shadows stretch.
- **Setting:** A castle corridor off the hall. Torches. A sign toward the dungeons one way, bathrooms the other. Noise fading behind them.
- **Who:** Harry and Ron running, robes, wands out now, Harry’s glasses flashing, Ron’s red hair. No one else sharp. A portrait watches them pass and does not tattle.
- **Faces:** Harry looks ahead, determined. Ron looks at Harry, keeping up, mouth tight. The portrait looks after them, worried.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C10-P2-P6

- **Beat:** A smell first. Then a shadow too big for the corridor.
- **Image:** They stop. Harry’s sleeve is over his nose. At the end of the corridor a shadow crosses that cannot belong to a person: a club, a lump of shoulder, a dragging arm. The torch flames lean away from it.
- **Setting:** A lower corridor near the bathrooms. Stone. A smell drawn as wavy lines and their faces. Torches. A girls’ bathroom door ahead, slightly open.
- **Who:** Harry, sleeve to his face, wand, glasses, scar. Ron beside him, freckles pale, wand shaking a little. The troll not fully seen, only the shadow, huge, gray-green at the edge of the light. The bathroom door.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the shadow, then at the bathroom door, fear and the reason they came. Ron looks at the shadow, throat working. Neither looks at the other. The shadow does not have a face yet.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "That smell. Ron, that is not a student."

### Page 3 — The bathroom

#### C10-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Hermione backed against the sinks. The troll fills the doorway.
- **Image:** The bathroom is white tile and fear. Hermione is small against a row of sinks. The troll’s body blocks the whole door, gray, lumpy, a club like a tree. She has nowhere to go.
- **Setting:** A girls’ bathroom. Sinks, mirrors, puddles, a cubicle door, harsh torch light from the corridor and cold white tile. Halloween night outside this room.
- **Who:** Hermione, school robes, bushy hair stuck to wet cheeks, book dropped in a puddle, both hands on the sink behind her. The troll, enormous, small head, tiny eyes, loincloth of something filthy, a great wooden club in one fist, bare gray feet. Harry and Ron are not in the room yet; the door is the troll.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks up at the troll, terrified, tears from earlier still on her face, a different fear now. The troll looks at her, dull, mouth open. It is not personal. That is worse.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Don't come in. It's in here with me."

#### C10-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Harry throws a tap. The troll turns.
- **Image:** Harry is in the doorway behind the troll, too small. A broken tap flies and dings the troll’s shoulder. The huge head turns. Hermione sees the boys and her fear changes shape.
- **Setting:** The bathroom. Water now spraying from the broken tap, uncontrolled. Mirrors. Puddles growing.
- **Who:** Harry, robes soaked at the hem, glasses, wand in one hand, the tap already thrown with the other. Ron just behind him, red hair, wand out, pale. The troll turning, club rising. Hermione at the sinks, recognizing them.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the troll’s turning head, committed. Ron looks at the club, swallowing. Hermione looks at Harry, shock and a flash of hope. The troll looks at Harry, annoyed, slow.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hey! Over here. Not her."

#### C10-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Ron’s wand shakes. He shouts the only spell he can remember.
- **Image:** Ron’s wand hand is a blur of nerves. His mouth is wide around a clumsy spell. The club, mid-swing toward Harry, shudders and lifts instead, yanked upward as if the troll’s own arm disagreed.
- **Setting:** Tile, spraying water, swinging club, tight and loud.
- **Who:** Ron, freckles, red hair plastered, maroon sweater dark with water, wand pointed, the other hand flung out for balance. Harry ducking under where the club was. The troll, confused, arm up. Hermione crawling clear along the sinks.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his own wand, astonished it worked, still shouting. Harry looks at the rising club, a window of a second. The troll looks at its arm, stupid and angry. Hermione looks at a cubicle she can reach.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Wingardium Leviosa!"

#### C10-P3-P4

- **Beat:** The club lifts and drops on the troll’s own head.
- **Image:** The payoff. The club comes down on the troll’s skull. Its eyes cross. Harry is under it, arms over his head. Water and dust burst. The panel is impact.
- **Setting:** The bathroom, mirrors cracking, water everywhere, tile.
- **Who:** The troll, club on its own head, knees buckling. Harry crouched, glasses, scar, robes. Ron with the wand still out, as if holding the spell by force. Hermione clear of the sinks now, watching, wet.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the troll, wild hope. Harry looks up through his arms. Hermione looks at Ron, amazed. The troll’s tiny eyes look at nothing, rolling.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C10-P3-P5

- **Beat:** The troll falls. Water and dust. Silence.
- **Image:** The troll is a fallen hill on the tile. The club rolls. Water from the broken tap patters, the only sound. The three children are standing in a triangle around it, too surprised to celebrate.
- **Setting:** The ruined bathroom. Cracked mirror, puddles, the huge body, torch smoke from the corridor.
- **Who:** The troll, face-down, gray, still. Harry, dripping, glasses spotted, wand lowered. Ron, sleeve torn from an older night and now soaked, wand down. Hermione, hair wrecked, robes wet, the book ruined in the puddle at her feet.
- **Faces:** They look at the troll, not at each other yet. Panting. Harry looks exhausted. Ron looks like he might be sick or laugh. Hermione looks at the troll, tears mixing with tap water, safe and shaking.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C10-P3-P6

- **Beat:** They are not heroic yet. They are alive.
- **Image:** A quiet close group. Hermione’s hand finds Ron’s sleeve, the one he tore weeks ago, a thank-you she does not have words for. Harry leans on a sink. Nobody poses.
- **Setting:** The bathroom, water still running, the troll huge and pathetic in the background. Steam or dust settling.
- **Who:** The three, wet, robes ruined, wands loose at their sides. Hermione’s fingers on Ron’s sleeve. Harry’s hand on the porcelain, head down. Ron staring at the club.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Ron, grateful, the common-room hurt not gone but set aside. Ron looks at her hand, ashamed and relieved. Harry looks at both of them, a small tired smile. Alive is enough.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "You came back for me." **Ron:** "Well. Yeah."

### Page 4 — The lie

#### C10-P4-P1

- **Beat:** McGonagall, Snape, and Quirrell burst in. McGonagall is white with anger.
- **Image:** The door fills with three teachers. McGonagall in front, face bloodless. Snape behind, eyes raking the room. Quirrell peering, turban straight again, handkerchief at his nose.
- **Setting:** The bathroom doorway. The fallen troll in full view. Water. Three soaked children.
- **Who:** McGonagall, emerald robes, bun coming loose, spectacles, no hat, fury. Snape, black, greasy hair, a limp he is hiding, one leg stiff. Quirrell, purple, turban, trembling. Harry, Ron, Hermione in a line, dripping.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at the troll, then at the children, anger born of fear. Snape looks at Harry, searching. Quirrell looks at the troll, faint again, or acting. Harry looks at McGonagall, bracing.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "What on earth were you thinking? A fully grown mountain troll. You might have been killed."

#### C10-P4-P2

- **Beat:** She turns on the boys. Hermione steps forward.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s finger is aimed at Harry and Ron. Hermione steps in front of it. She is smaller and she makes the finger irrelevant. Ron’s mouth is open to confess and stops.
- **Setting:** The wet tile. The troll behind like evidence.
- **Who:** McGonagall, emerald, finger out. Hermione, wet hair, chin up, stepping between. Harry and Ron behind her, stunned by the step. Snape watching, black, unconvinced already. Quirrell hovering at the back.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Hermione, the anger pausing. Hermione looks up at her, determined, about to lie for them. Harry looks at the back of Hermione’s head, startled. Ron looks at her, guilty that she is doing this.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Explain yourselves, Potter, Weasley." **Hermione:** "Professor, wait. Please."

#### C10-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione says she went looking for the troll, and the boys came to save her.
- **Image:** Hermione, hands spread, selling the lie with a steady voice. She does not look at the boys, because she might laugh or cry. McGonagall’s face shifts from fury to a different disappointment.
- **Setting:** Close three-shot. The bathroom soft behind. Water drips off Hermione’s hair.
- **Who:** Hermione, soaked robes, ruined book forgotten, empty hands, the lie held carefully. McGonagall listening, spectacles a little fogged. Harry and Ron statues behind, not contradicting her.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at McGonagall, earnest, brave in a new way. A flicker of fear she will not be believed. McGonagall looks at her, reading it, choosing to allow it. Harry looks at the floor, honoring the lie by silence.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I went looking for the troll. I thought I could manage it. They came to stop me being killed. The fault is mine."

#### C10-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Points off Hermione, points to the boys. Snape looks unconvinced.
- **Image:** McGonagall’s hands move like a scale: a deduction toward Hermione, awards toward the boys. Snape’s mouth is a cut. He does not clap. Quirrell does, weakly, and looks at Snape.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the bathroom now, the door shut on the troll. Torches. Wet footprints.
- **Who:** McGonagall, composed again, emerald, delivering the points. Hermione, punished and proud. Harry and Ron, uncomfortable with being rewarded. Snape, black, weight on his good leg, eyes on Harry. Quirrell, turban, hands fluttering.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Hermione, stern affection. Hermione looks back, accepting the points loss. Snape looks at Harry, he does not believe a word, and he looks at Quirrell once, colder. Quirrell looks at the floor. Harry looks at Snape’s leg.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "Five points from Gryffindor, Miss Granger, for foolishness. Five points each to Potter and Weasley, for sheer nerve." **Snape:** "How very convenient a story."

#### C10-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Leaving, Harry sees blood soaking through Snape’s trouser leg.
- **Image:** As Snape turns, his black robe swings and shows a dark wet patch at the calf, too red to be water. Harry’s eyes lock on it. Snape’s hand twitches the robe closed a second late.
- **Setting:** The torchlit corridor. The others walking ahead. A private piece of seeing.
- **Who:** Snape from behind and side, black robes, greasy hair, the injured leg, one hand catching the cloth. Harry a step back, glasses, scar, wet, staring. Ron and Hermione ahead, not seeing. Quirrell far, turban bobbing.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the blood, suspicion clicking into place, mouth closed. Snape does not look back, but his shoulders know he was seen. His profile, if caught, is pain held very still.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "His leg. That's blood, not water."

#### C10-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Later the three sit together. Thanks, muttered both ways. They are friends.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The common room, late, almost empty. Three chairs pulled close to the fire. Hermione has a towel on her hair. Ron is saying thank you into his pumpkin juice. She is saying it back into her knees. Harry watches them and the fire, and the group is a triangle that will hold.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, night, fire, Halloween over. Quiet. A clock. Red hangings.
- **Who:** Hermione, towel, dry robes, bushy hair escaping, a mug. Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, ears still red, a mug. Harry, glasses, scar, dry, a blanket on his shoulders, mug in both hands. No one else.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at his mug, then at Hermione, shy thanks. Hermione looks at the fire, then at Ron, the insult forgiven if not forgotten, a small smile. Harry looks at both of them, content, the suspicion about Snape set aside for this one warm panel. They look like friends.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Thanks. For the lie." **Hermione:** "Thanks for the troll. Friends, then."

---

## Chapter 12 — Quidditch

Nerves, a cursed broom, a small fire, the Snitch, and a name Hagrid did not mean to say.

### Page 1 — Before the whistle

#### C11-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The changing room. Harry’s robes are too big. Wood kneels with the plan.
- **Image:** A cramped stone changing room. Harry stands in Quidditch robes that pool at his shoes. Wood kneels, drawing the plan on a bench with a wet finger. The team is a wall of older bodies.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor changing room. Benches, pads, a high window, the roar of a crowd already outside, muffled. Red and gold everywhere.
- **Who:** Harry, small, messy hair, glasses, scar, scarlet Quidditch robes too long, gloves too big, a broom — a clean fast one, Nimbus — beside him. Oliver Wood, brown hair, captain’s badge, arm guard, kneeling. The twins Fred and George in Beater gear, identical grins. Other teammates, Alicia, Katie, Angelina, older, kind, tall.
- **Faces:** Wood looks up at Harry, intense, trying to pour a whole game into him. Harry looks at the finger-plan, swallowing, brave and new. Fred looks at George, a reassuring joke. They look at Harry, friendly.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Chasers mind the Quaffle. Beaters mind the Bludgers. You, Potter, watch the sky for the Snitch and nothing else."

#### C11-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Catch the Snitch and they cannot lose. Wood’s eyes are not sure a first year can.
- **Image:** Close. Wood’s hands on Harry’s shoulders, steadying the too-big robes. His mouth says the simple plan. His eyes flick to Harry’s size and back, hiding the doubt badly.
- **Setting:** The bench, the roar nearer. A whistle somewhere. Leather and grass smell at the door.
- **Who:** Wood, captain, brown hair, hands on the small shoulders. Harry, gloves, glasses, scar, the broom now in his grip.
- **Faces:** Wood looks at Harry, willing him to be taller. Harry looks back, he heard the doubt and he nods anyway. A tight brave smile.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Catch the Golden Snitch and they cannot catch our score. I am trusting a first year, and a Nimbus Two Thousand. Do not make me regret either."

#### C11-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Harry walks out. The stadium is a roar.
- **Image:** The tunnel mouth. Dark, then a blast of light and sound. Harry is a silhouette becoming a small scarlet figure. The stands are a moving texture of houses.
- **Setting:** The Quidditch pitch, daytime, packed wooden stands, banners, the castle beyond, a bright cold sky. Four goal hoops at each end.
- **Who:** Harry stepping out, too-big robes, broom in hand, glasses, scar, gloves. Wood ahead of him. The crowd, including Ron and Hermione in the Gryffindor stand, tiny. Hagrid huge even at a distance, a flower behind his ear or just his beard. McGonagall in the staff seats. Snape. Quirrell’s turban.
- **Faces:** Harry looks up at the roar, eyes wide, a step hitching then landing. He looks at the sky, which he trusts more than the crowd. Wood looks back, a nod.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C11-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Green team below. Draco points from the stands.
- **Image:** Slytherin in green, already mounted, looking expensive and mean. In the stands Draco is on his feet, arm out, pointing at Harry so the people around him will look and laugh.
- **Setting:** The pitch and the Slytherin section. Green and silver banners. The grass marked.
- **Who:** Slytherin players, green robes, brooms, a larger Seeker sneering. Draco, platinum hair, school robes, pointing, Crabbe and Goyle laughing beside him. Harry small on the pitch, looking up by accident.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at Harry, mocking, mouth open in a shout Harry cannot hear. The Slytherin Seeker looks at Harry, dismissing him. Harry looks at Draco for one second, then away, at his broom. He will not play to the point.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "Look at the scarf on legs! Gryffindor's Seeker still needs a growth charm!"

#### C11-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Hooch throws the balls up. Harry kicks off.
- **Image:** Madam Hooch’s arm flings the Quaffle. Two Bludgers and the tiny gold Snitch blast upward. Harry kicks off in the same instant, a clean rise. The teams follow.
- **Setting:** The center circle. Bright grass. The sky opening. Balls like exclamation points.
- **Who:** Madam Hooch, yellow eyes, whistle in her teeth, dark cloak, arm extended. Harry rising, scarlet, broom, body remembering the courtyard. Wood shouting. Fred and George up with bats. The Snitch a gold speck already gone.
- **Faces:** Hooch looks at the balls, fierce and fair. Harry looks up, the fear burned off, focus arriving. His mouth is slightly open. Joy under the job.
- **Dialogue:** **Madam Hooch:** "Mount your brooms. Three, two, one, brooms up!"

#### C11-P1-P6

- **Beat:** From above, the pitch is a diagram and Harry is a speck.
- **Image:** Bird’s eye. The pitch is Wood’s diagram come alive: hoops, tiny players, the crowd a ring. Harry is one scarlet speck circling higher than the game.
- **Setting:** Daytime stadium, castle, lake, the Forbidden Forest a dark edge. Wind, implied by the lean of banners.
- **Who:** Everyone tiny. Harry’s speck apart from the knot of Chasers. The Snitch not visible, which is the point. Snape’s black speck in the staff stand. Hermione’s speck already standing up.
- **Faces:** None readable. The picture is scale. Harry is small and the sky is his.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

### Page 2 — The jinx

#### C11-P2-P1

- **Beat:** He hunts for a glint of gold. A Bludger screams past.
- **Image:** Harry banks. A black iron Bludger tears past his ear, a blur with a face like a cannonball. He does not chase it. His eyes search the air for gold.
- **Setting:** Mid-air. The crowd a smear. A goal hoop. Sun in his eyes.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet robes streaming, gloves, glasses, broom, body low over the handle. The Bludger. George or Fred far below, bat swinging, red hair.
- **Faces:** Harry looks past the Bludger, not at it, hunting. Concentration. A flicker of thrill. The Beater looks up at him, protective.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C11-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The broom bucks. He clamps his knees.
- **Image:** The broom jerks like a living thing bitten. Harry’s knees lock. One hand stays on, the other flies up. His glasses slip.
- **Setting:** Open air above the center. The game continuing below, not seeing him yet.
- **Who:** Harry, small on a suddenly wild broom, scarlet cloth, messy hair, glasses crooked, scar. The broom, fine wood, misbehaving.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the broom, startled, then at his own hands. The joy is gone. Confusion first, then the start of fear.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Steady. What are you doing? Steady!"

#### C11-P2-P3

- **Beat:** It twists harder. One hand slips off.
- **Image:** He is sideways. One hand is off. The broom rolls. The ground is a green threat. The crowd has seen now; a thousand faces tilt up.
- **Setting:** High above the pitch. Banners. A collective gasp drawn as open mouths in the stands, small.
- **Who:** Harry hanging half-off, one glove locked, the other grabbing air, robes over his head partly, glasses barely on. Wood below, looking up, the game forgotten. Hooch’s whistle frozen.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the ground, real fear, teeth set, not screaming. Wood looks up, horrified. He looks at the teachers, wanting a stop.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Potter's in trouble! The broom's fighting him!"

#### C11-P2-P4

- **Beat:** In the stands, Hermione tracks the magic with her eyes.
- **Image:** Hermione is standing on the bench, hair whipped by wind, eyes narrowed, following an invisible line from the air down to the staff seats. Ron is pulling her sleeve, panicked about Harry, not about clues.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor stand. Scarves, shouting, Hagrid a few rows away, huge, upset, fists clenched. The pitch far below in the angle of the panel, Harry a tumbling speck.
- **Who:** Hermione, school robes, red-and-gold scarf, binoculars in one hand she is not using because she is looking at people, not the broom. Ron, scarf, freckles, white-faced, both hands on the rail. Hagrid, moleskin coat, devastated.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the staff, detective-sharp, scared. Ron looks only at Harry, begging the air. Hagrid looks at Harry, helpless, eyes wet.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's a jinx. Someone is jinxing his broom." **Ron:** "Then find them, don't explain them!"

#### C11-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Snape is motionless, staring at Harry, mouth moving.
- **Image:** The staff stand, still amid screaming people. Snape sits rigid. His eyes are locked upward. His lips move. One hand is hidden in his robe. McGonagall beside him is standing, shouting for order. He does not stand.
- **Setting:** The teachers’ box. Benches, a railing, the pitch beyond. Quirrell is there too, turban, seemingly fidgeting, easy to miss because Snape is so still.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, greasy hair, sallow face, unmoving body, moving mouth. McGonagall, emerald, on her feet, spectacles, alarmed. Quirrell, purple turban, hands in his lap, also looking up, also mouthing, overlooked. Dumbledore is not in this crop.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at Harry, intense, no blink. It reads as an attack. McGonagall looks at the sky, afraid for the boy. Quirrell looks up, a bead of sweat, his stare the true one if anyone were looking at him. No one is.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "Hold fast. Contra the jinx. Do not throw him."

#### C11-P2-P6

- **Beat:** She shoves through the crowd toward the staff seats.
- **Image:** Hermione is a determined wedge through scarves and elbows. Ron is behind her, confused, still looking back at the sky. She does not explain. Her wand is already in her fist.
- **Setting:** The packed stand, stairs, house colors, the staff box getting closer. Noise.
- **Who:** Hermione, scarf flying, bushy hair, wand out, face set. Ron stumbling after, red hair. Spectators turning, annoyed. Hagrid shifting his bulk to let her pass, trusting her without knowing why.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Snape’s box, fierce. Ron looks at her, lost, then up at Harry, torn. A spectator looks at her wand, alarmed. Hagrid looks at her, hopeful.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "It's Snape. He's staring at Harry and his mouth hasn't stopped. Come on."

### Page 3 — Fire and the Snitch

#### C11-P3-P1

- **Beat:** A small blue flame catches Snape’s hem.
- **Image:** Hermione leans over a railing and a tiny precise blue fire bites the hem of Snape’s black robe. It is small and rude and perfect. His concentration snaps.
- **Setting:** The edge of the staff seats. Wood railing. The crowd behind Hermione. Snape’s robes a black pool.
- **Who:** Hermione, wand tip glowing, scarf, determination. Snape, seated, the flame on his hem, eyes breaking from the sky. Ron behind her, suddenly understanding, a gasp. Quirrell flinching as the counter-jinx — unseen — dies, a sweat drop, not noticed.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the flame, not gloating, urgent. Snape looks down, shock and fury. Ron looks at the fire, horrified and impressed. Harry is not in frame. The sky is winning off-panel.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Lacarnum inflamari."

#### C11-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Snape beats the robe. The broom goes still.
- **Image:** Split feeling: Snape smacking his hem with both hands, standing, black hair falling; and far off, Harry’s broom leveling so suddenly he almost floats.
- **Setting:** Staff box and open air, one panel with a clear distance between them.
- **Who:** Snape, black robes, smoking hem, furious hands. McGonagall looking at the hem, suspicious of Hermione more than of him. Harry in the air, one hand on, body unfolding back onto the broom. Hermione ducking below the railing.
- **Faces:** Snape looks at his robe, then snaps his head toward the stands, rage. Harry looks at the broom, a sob of relief that is not quite a smile. Hermione looks up from hiding, checking him. McGonagall looks at the spot Hermione was, eyes narrowed.
- **Dialogue:** **Snape:** "My robes! Who set fire to my robes?"

#### C11-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry hangs one-handed. Something gold brushes his lips.
- **Image:** He is still recovering, hanging, and the Snitch — tiny, gold, silver wings — flicks against his mouth as if choosing him. His eyes cross trying to see it.
- **Setting:** Air, sun, the gold insect-bright against his face. The pitch far below, game resumed in miniature.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet, glasses, one glove on the broom, the other hand too far, the Snitch at his lips. No one else near. Seekers are supposed to chase. This one arrives.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the Snitch, cross-eyed, astonishment. His mouth opens on instinct. The Snitch is all wings and will.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C11-P3-P4

- **Beat:** He hits the grass. The crowd thinks he has fallen.
- **Image:** A thud of scarlet on the grass. The broom beside him. The crowd is on its feet in the wrong kind of silence. Dust. He is curled, both hands at his mouth.
- **Setting:** The pitch, center, bright day. Players landing. Wood running. The stands a wall of alarm.
- **Who:** Harry on the ground, robes muddy now as well as too big, glasses askew, hands over his mouth. Wood running, captain’s badge, fear. Hooch approaching. The Slytherin Seeker circling, confused.
- **Faces:** The crowd looks down, afraid. Wood looks at Harry, panicked. Harry’s eyes are open, looking up at them, and there is a secret in his cheeks. He has not shown it.
- **Dialogue:** **Lee Jordan:** "Potter's down! Gryffindor's Seeker is down!"

#### C11-P3-P5

- **Beat:** He pulls the Snitch out of his mouth.
- **Image:** Close. Mud, a grin, and the golden Snitch held up between two fingers, wings beating against his pinch. His lip is cut a little. It is perfect.
- **Setting:** Grass and sky. The roar about to return. Sun on gold.
- **Who:** Harry, filthy scarlet, glasses, scar, hair full of grass, the Snitch in his fingers. His other hand braces him up.
- **Faces:** He looks at the Snitch, then past it at the stands, a dazed laugh. Triumph, disbelief, a child’s joy. The Snitch looks caught and furious, wings blurring.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "I've got it. I've got the Snitch!"

#### C11-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Gryffindor erupts. Wood lifts him. Harry is dazed and laughing.
- **Image:** Wood hoists him. The twins crash in. The stand is a red explosion. Scarves. Hermione and Ron screaming. Hagrid pounding his knees. The Snitch still in Harry’s fist above the tangle.
- **Setting:** The pitch and the Gryffindor stand, one celebration across the panel. Banners. Daylight.
- **Who:** Harry lifted, muddy, laughing, Snitch held high. Wood, brown hair, yelling. Fred and George, bats dropped. Ron and Hermione in the stand, scarves, faces open. Hagrid roaring with happiness. Far in the staff box, Snape sitting again, hem singed, not cheering. Draco on his feet, not cheering.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron and Hermione, laughing, finding them in the crowd. Wood looks at Harry, the doubt gone, delighted. Snape looks at Harry, cold, the corner of his mouth tight. Draco looks at the Snitch, sour.
- **Dialogue:** **Wood:** "Seeker! You mad, beautiful Seeker!" **Ron:** "He caught it in his mouth!"

### Page 4 — The name

#### C11-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Hagrid’s hut that night. Tea, clutter, the three of them.
- **Image:** The hut is one warm room against the dark grounds. A table, enormous mugs, rock cakes that could be weapons, a fire. Fang the boarhound asleep, huge and harmless. The three children small in big chairs.
- **Setting:** Hagrid’s hut, night. A window onto the black lawn. Herbs hanging. A crossbow on the wall. The fire the main light. Cozy and slightly dangerous, like Hagrid.
- **Who:** Hagrid, coat off, a huge knit vest, beard, pouring tea from a kettle like a cauldron. Harry, school robes, a bruise of a grin still, glasses, scar, mug in both hands. Ron, freckles, maroon sweater, eyeing a rock cake with respect. Hermione, scarf off, books even here, mug. Fang, a black boarhound, head on paws.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at Harry, proud, misty. Harry looks at the tea, happy and tired. Ron looks at the cake, wary. Hermione looks at Hagrid, fond, and already steering toward a question.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Tea. And don't ask for seconds of the rock cakes. They're a weapon."

#### C11-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape tried to kill him. Hagrid says Snape would not.
- **Image:** Harry leaning forward, the game in his hands as he retells it. Hagrid’s big hand waves the idea off, sloshing tea. Hermione watches Hagrid’s face more than Harry’s story.
- **Setting:** The table, firelight, Fang’s ear twitching.
- **Who:** Harry, earnest, glasses reflecting fire. Hagrid, vest, beard, dismissive gentle hand. Hermione, sharp. Ron nodding with Harry, loyal to the theory.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, sure, a little hurt that he is not believed. Hagrid looks at Harry, troubled but firm, he likes Snape enough to doubt this, or he fears the subject. Ron looks at Hagrid, disagreeing. Hermione looks at Hagrid, noticing what he will not say.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Snape tried to kill me. He was jinxing the broom." **Hagrid:** "Professor Snape? He wouldn't. Not to a student. Not to you."

#### C11-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Hermione describes the muttering. Hagrid will not discuss the third floor.
- **Image:** Hermione, precise, imitates a still man moving his mouth. Hagrid’s eyes flick to the window, toward the castle’s third floor, then he shuts down. He stands to fetch more tea he does not need.
- **Setting:** The hut. Through the window, the castle lit, one dark band of floor. Night.
- **Who:** Hermione, hands demonstrating. Hagrid, back half-turned, kettle, shoulders up. Harry watching the window because Hagrid did. Ron mid-bite of a rock cake, regretting it.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Hagrid, not accusing, exact. Hagrid looks at the kettle, avoiding, beard hiding his mouth. Harry looks at the castle, the dog and the package waking up. Ron looks at the cake, betrayed by it.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "He never looked away, and he was muttering the whole match." **Hagrid:** "I am not talking about the third floor. Drop it."

#### C11-P4-P4
