- **Who:** The unicorn, dead, slender legs folded wrong, mane in the blood. Harry and Draco at the edge, small, cloaks, the lantern useless against that shine. No attacker yet.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the unicorn, grief, a hand half-up as if he could still help. His mouth is open. Draco looks at it, the pure-blood boy who knows the weight of this, genuinely shaken. The sneer is gone. Fear is clean on him.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's a unicorn. It's dead. That shine is unicorn blood."

#### C15-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Draco makes a small sound and steps back.
- **Image:** Draco’s foot slips in moss. He steps back into a tree, one hand up, the sound small and childlike. Harry does not mock him. Harry is not looking at him. Harry is looking at the far side of the clearing, where the pool is disturbed.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. Silver light. A dark gap in the trees opposite.
- **Who:** Draco, platinum hair, cloak, back to a trunk, pale. Harry, lantern lowered, glasses, scar beginning to prick, body still. The unicorn in the foreground, still.
- **Faces:** Draco looks at the blood, then at the dark gap, a whimper he hates. Harry looks at the gap, focused, the scar a warning. He looks calm and he is not.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "I don't like this. Potter, I really don't like this."

#### C15-P2-P4

- **Beat:** A hooded figure crouched over the body, drinking.
- **Image:** The figure is there, as if it was always in the dark and the dark sat up. A cloak with a hood, no face, bent to the unicorn’s throat, silver blood at the hood’s edge, drinking. It is the most wrong thing in a forest of wrong things.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The silver pool. The hooded shape breaking it.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tattered dark cloak, hunched, hands like pale spiders, face hidden, mouth at the wound. The unicorn. Harry and Draco small at the edge, Harry’s lantern shaking a fraction.
- **Faces:** No face in the hood, only a blackness and a glint. Harry looks at it, horror, scar blazing under his hair. Draco looks at it, the last of his posture gone. The figure does not look at them yet. It is feeding.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C15-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Harry’s scar tears. He drops to a knee.
- **Image:** Pain is a white crack across the panel. Harry is down on one knee, hand clamped to his forehead, lantern fallen, its light rolling. The hood lifts because the pain called it, or because he made a sound.
- **Setting:** Moss, silver blood nearby, the lantern on its side. The clearing tilting.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak, glasses askew, both knees wanting to go, one hand on the scar, the other in the moss. Draco standing but barely, behind him. The hooded figure straightening.
- **Faces:** Harry’s eyes are squeezed, pain, he looks at the ground not the figure. Draco looks at Harry, then at the hood, and the calculation is simple: run. The hood looks toward Harry. The glint in it finds him.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "My scar. Draco, my scar is splitting open."

#### C15-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The hood lifts. No readable face. Draco runs.
- **Image:** The hood comes up. What is under it is not a face the panel will explain: a blur, a paleness, a wrongness, Voldemort not restored. Draco breaks. He runs back down the path, cloak flying, platinum hair, abandoning Harry without a speech. Harry is still on one knee.
- **Setting:** The clearing. The path back. Silver light. The figure between Harry and the unicorn.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, tall now, cloak, unreadable head, a step toward Harry. Draco running, back to us, fast, cowardice and survival the same motion. Harry kneeling, glasses, scar, lantern out of reach.
- **Faces:** Draco does not look back. His back is the expression: panic. Harry looks up at the hood, unable to stand, seeing nothing he can name. The hood looks at Harry, and the scar answers.
- **Dialogue:** **Draco:** "I am not staying for that. I am not."

### Page 3 — Firenze

#### C15-P3-P1

- **Beat:** The figure glides toward Harry. He cannot stand.
- **Image:** The hooded thing does not walk so much as arrive closer. Harry’s legs will not obey. Silver blood on the figure’s mouth, or where a mouth would be. Harry’s hand finds his wand and it feels like a twig.
- **Setting:** The clearing, fog torn by movement. The unicorn behind the figure like an accusation.
- **Who:** The hooded figure, cloak dragging through the silver pool, pale hands out. Harry on the ground, school cloak, wand in a shaking hand, glasses, scar white-hot. No Draco. No lantern steady.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the hands, terror held down by pain. He looks very small. The hood looks at him with a hunger that is not only for blood. No readable features, so the hunger is in the lean of the body.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C15-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Hooves. A centaur blocks the hood.
- **Image:** Hooves crash into the clearing. A centaur rears between Harry and the hood, a living wall: human torso, horse body, bow up, hair flying. The hood recoils from him, not from Harry’s wand.
- **Setting:** The clearing, suddenly violent with motion. Moss torn. Silver splashed.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino body, cream-gold coat, bare chest, long blond hair, a bow, stern young face, hooves between Harry and death. The hooded figure shrinking back. Harry on the ground behind the centaur’s legs, looking up at a savior he did not expect.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the hood, cold, ancient anger, no fear. The hood looks at him, reluctant, hungry, retreating. Harry looks at Firenze’s back, shock and relief crashing together.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Back, foul thing. You will not drink and hunt in the same hour."

#### C15-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Firenze tells Harry to climb on. Harry does.
- **Image:** Firenze kneels, impossible grace, and Harry scrambles onto the horse back, clumsy, fists in the pale mane. The hood is at the tree line, not gone, watching.
- **Setting:** The clearing’s edge. The unicorn still. The path away.
- **Who:** Firenze, kneeling, blond hair, a hand back to help, bow in the other. Harry, cloak tangled, glasses, swinging a leg up, small on the broad back. The hooded figure, paused between trees.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks toward the path, urgent, calm voice, he does not look afraid but he will not linger. Harry looks at the mane, then at the hood, he does not want to turn his back and he does. The hood looks at Harry being taken, a promise more than a glare.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Harry Potter. Climb on. Now."

#### C15-P3-P4

- **Beat:** They move. The hood does not follow past the centaur.
- **Image:** The forest streaks. Firenze gallops. Harry clings. Behind, at the edge of the silver clearing, the hood stands still, unwilling to cross some line the centaur carries with him. Then the trees take the view.
- **Setting:** The forest at speed. Trunks, fog, mane, the clearing shrinking.
- **Who:** Firenze running, powerful, hair and Harry’s cloak streaming together. Harry bent low, arms around him as much as pride allows, glasses pressed to his face. The hood small and still behind.
- **Faces:** Harry looks back once, the scar easing with distance, fear still high. Firenze looks ahead, profile stern, he does not look back. The hood looks after them, patient. It can wait. It has been waiting.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C15-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Firenze says he has never seen a unicorn die like that.
- **Image:** They have stopped at a stream. Firenze’s head is bowed, the sternness cracked by grief. Harry slides down, legs unsteady. The forest is quiet in a mourning way.
- **Setting:** A moonlit stream, safer trees, water over stones, no silver blood. The castle is still far.
- **Who:** Firenze, palomino, blond hair shadowing his face, bow lowered. Harry, cloak torn at the hem by branches, glasses, scar faded from white-hot to sore, standing small beside a centaur.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the water, grieved, angry. Harry looks at him, the question waiting in his mouth. He looks young and polite and badly shaken. He does not rush the centaur’s grief.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "I have never seen a unicorn slain like that. Their blood is a terrible thing to spill."

#### C15-P3-P6

- **Beat:** Harry, still holding the scar, asks what that thing was.
- **Image:** His fingers are on the scar again, a habit now. He looks up the long height of Firenze. The question is the panel. An owl. The stream.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Moon. A centaur and a boy.
- **Who:** Harry, school cloak, pajamas or clothes under it from detention, glasses, scar under his fingers. Firenze, bare chest, horse body, bow, looking down.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, needing the name, afraid of it. Firenze looks at Harry, measuring whether a child should hear this. His face is kind and severe. He decides to tell.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "What was under that hood? My scar knew it before I did."

### Page 4 — The curse

#### C15-P4-P1

- **Beat:** Unicorn blood will keep you alive an inch from death.
- **Image:** Firenze speaks. In the air between them, Harry imagines it without a full vision panel: the hood at the unicorn’s throat, the silver keeping a ruined thing in the world. Harry’s hand stays on his scar.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon on the water. Quiet.
- **Who:** Firenze, gesturing once, restrained. Harry listening, cloak wrapped tight, glasses, small.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, unwilling teacher. Harry looks at him, the horror settling into sense. He looks ill. He looks angry on the unicorn’s behalf.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "Unicorn blood will hold you in the world, even an inch from death. That is why the hooded thing was drinking."

#### C15-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The price is a half-life, cursed from the moment you drink.
- **Image:** Firenze’s face, close, the sentence landing. Harry takes a step back without meaning to, not from Firenze, from the idea. The stream goes on, clean, a contrast with cursed blood.
- **Setting:** Moonlight, water, trees.
- **Who:** Firenze’s face, blond hair, solemn eyes. Harry, a step off, cloak, the scar.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at Harry, pity and warning. Harry looks at the ground, imagining a half-life, then back up, braver. He asks with his eyes: who would pay that.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "The price is a half-life. Cursed, from the first swallow. A monster's bargain."

#### C15-P4-P3

- **Beat:** Harry says the name he is afraid of. Firenze does not correct him.
- **Image:** Harry says it. The trees do not like it. Firenze’s ears — human — do not flinch into denial. A leaf falls. The name is accepted. That is the answer.
- **Setting:** The stream bank. Stillness after a dangerous word.
- **Who:** Harry, mouth just closed on the name, glasses, cloak, fists. Firenze, towering, a short nod.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Firenze, hoping to be told he is wrong. Firenze looks at him, grave, he will not give that comfort. Harry’s face changes: the hope closes. Knowledge arrives.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It was Voldemort." **Firenze:** "I will not correct you."

#### C15-P4-P4

- **Beat:** Voldemort is in these trees because of the Stone.
- **Image:** Firenze turns his head toward the castle, far through the trees, a few lights. The line from the forest to the school is the story. Harry follows the look. The Stone is in there. The thing in the hood wants it.
- **Setting:** A gap in the trees. Distant castle windows. The forest vast around a small boy and a centaur.
- **Who:** Firenze, profile, blond hair, bow, looking toward Hogwarts. Harry beside the palomino shoulder, looking the same way, suddenly seeing the castle as a vault.
- **Faces:** Firenze looks at the castle, troubled for the world, not only the boy. Harry looks at the lights, the dog, the package, the book, all connecting. His face is pale and certain.
- **Dialogue:** **Firenze:** "He walks these trees because of the Philosopher's Stone. It is hidden in your school."

#### C15-P4-P5

- **Beat:** The Stone would give Voldemort a body without the curse.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands open and close. He understands the theft. Not gold. A body. A return. The hood would not need unicorn blood if it had the Stone. He looks at his own hands, the boy who already survived that return once.
- **Setting:** The stream. Moon. Close on him, Firenze a tall presence at the edge.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, cloak, empty hands, the understanding. Firenze watching him, not interrupting.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hands, then at the scar, fear and a fierce no. Firenze looks at him, respect, a child who understood quickly. Sadness, because understanding is not safety.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "The Stone would give him a body. He would not need the curse of the blood." **Firenze:** "You understand quickly. I am sorry that you do."

#### C15-P4-P6

- **Beat:** Castle lights ahead. He looks at his friends, sure now that Snape serves the hood.
- **Image:** Page-turn. They have reached the forest edge. Hermione and Neville and Hagrid run up, lanterns bouncing. Draco is already there, pale, not meeting eyes. Harry looks at his friends and does not say the centaur’s whole speech. His face says he has a villain. He is wrong about the face, and he is sure.
- **Setting:** The forest edge, the castle blazing with ordinary windows, safety looking close and false. Night. Mist.
- **Who:** Harry, cloak torn, glasses, scar, Firenze already fading back into the trees, a nod goodbye. Hermione reaching him, hair, lantern, fear for him. Neville behind her, crying with relief. Hagrid, huge, crossbow, devastated that Harry was alone. Draco apart, cloak muddy, looking at the ground. Fang licking Harry’s hand.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hermione, alive, and past her toward the castle, the theory locked: Snape. Hermione looks at Harry, scanning him for wounds. Hagrid looks at the trees, guilty. Draco looks at no one, shame. Firenze looks back once, unreadable, and the dark takes him.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Harry. Are you hurt?" **Harry:** "I'm all right. It was Voldemort. And Snape is helping him. I know it."

## Chapter 17 — They decide to protect the Stone

Exams, Hagrid’s slip, a warning refused, and a flute in a dark corridor.

### Page 1 — Exams and a slip

#### C16-P1-P1

- **Beat:** The exam hall. Harry writes, and thinks about the forest.
- **Image:** Rows of desks. Sun. Harry’s parchment has a paragraph and then a blank, because his quill has stopped. In his head the hood is still drinking. A bead of ink drops.
- **Setting:** The Great Hall turned into an exam room. Morning. Invigilators. Floating clocks. Summer at the windows, green and unfair.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, tie, glasses, scar, quill stalled. Hermione two desks away, already on the second page, hair, perfect handwriting. Ron biting his quill. McGonagall prowling, emerald, spectacles.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the parchment, not seeing the question. He looks haunted and ordinary. Hermione looks at her work, focused, a glance at him that is worry. McGonagall looks at the room, fair and watchful.
- **Dialogue:** No dialogue.

#### C16-P1-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione finishes early and checks the page twice.
- **Image:** She sets her quill down, then picks it up, then sets it down. Checking is how she loves people and essays. She looks across at Harry’s blank lines and does not pass him an answer. Her foot taps.
- **Setting:** The exam hall. A clock. Summer light.
- **Who:** Hermione, finished parchment, neat, robes, a smudge she resents on one finger. Harry still stalled. Ron still biting, a doodle in the margin he will regret.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her last line, critical, then at Harry, soft. She looks back at her page, discipline. Harry does not look up. His face is in the forest.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Last line again. Then once more, to be sure."

#### C16-P1-P3

- **Beat:** Ron stares at a question and accidentally draws a dragon.
- **Image:** The corner of Ron’s parchment. A dragon, quite good, smoking. The actual answer is one line and doubtful. He stares at the question as if it bit him. The bandage is gone; a pale scar of a bite remains on his hand.
- **Setting:** His desk. Sun. McGonagall’s shadow about to pass.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, ink on his nose, the dragon doodle, quill. His bitten hand, healed, resting on the paper.
- **Faces:** He looks at the question, despair, then at the dragon, a private grin, then panic as the shadow nears. He looks like a boy at the end of a long year.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "What is the difference between monkshood and... oh, forget the question."

#### C16-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Later, Hagrid’s hut. He weeps into a handkerchief the size of a sail.
- **Image:** Exams are over. The hut again. Hagrid is at the table, sobbing, a handkerchief like a tablecloth at his face. The children came to celebrate and found a crisis. Tea is poured and ignored.
- **Setting:** The hut, late afternoon, summer, door open, Fang’s head on Harry’s knee. No egg. No dragon. The absence is loud.
- **Who:** Hagrid, vest, beard wet, the huge handkerchief. Harry, Ron, Hermione in lighter clothes under open robes, end of term. Fang, mournful because Hagrid is.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks into the cloth, guilty, wrecked. Harry looks at him, alarm replacing the exam haze. Hermione looks at Hagrid, gentle, ready to ask. Ron looks at the empty hearth, understanding this is about Norbert or worse.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "I should never have said a word. Great stupid mouth on me."

#### C16-P1-P5

- **Beat:** He says he should never have told. The three go still.
- **Image:** The sentence drops. Three teacups stop. Fang’s tail stops. Hagrid looks up, red-eyed, realizing he has said the outside of the secret.
- **Setting:** The table. Afternoon light. Stillness.
- **Who:** Hagrid, handkerchief lowered, vest, miserable. The three, cups, eyes up.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at them, regret. Harry looks at him, the forest and the Stone rising fast. Hermione looks at him, careful, she will not pounce. Ron looks at Harry, here we go. All of them very still.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "I told. I told a stranger how to get past Fluffy."

#### C16-P1-P6

- **Beat:** Harry asks, very carefully, told what.
- **Image:** Harry’s hands are flat on the table, a boy trying not to spook a wounded animal. His voice is the quietest thing in the hut. Hagrid’s mouth works. The handkerchief twists.
- **Setting:** Close across the table. Mugs. Fang’s eye. Summer.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, open robes, careful hands. Hagrid, huge and reduced, beard, twisting cloth.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Hagrid, kind and relentless. Hagrid looks at the handkerchief, then at Harry, he wants to be stopped and he will not be. Trust and dread.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Told what, Hagrid? Slowly."

### Page 2 — The stranger

#### C16-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A man in a pub kept buying the drinks and kept Hagrid talking.
- **Image:** A memory, softer edges. The Hog’s Head or the Leaky Cauldron, a dark corner, a hooded stranger pushing a mug across a wet table. Hagrid, flushed, talking with his hands. Cards or a game nearby. The stranger’s face is in shadow on purpose.
- **Setting:** A grimy wizard pub, memory-colored. Smoke. A hood. Two mugs.
- **Who:** Memory-Hagrid, coat, beard, too many empty mugs, earnest. The stranger, hood up, hands pale, one hand pushing a drink, face hidden. Not clearly turban or Snape. Ambiguous, which is honest to Hagrid’s memory.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the stranger, lonely and glad of the company, unsuspecting. The stranger looks down, listening too well. We do not get eyes.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Fluffy's mine. Raised him from a pup. Three heads, and gentle if you know the trick." **Stranger:** "Another firewhisky. Do tell me about the school."

#### C16-P2-P2

- **Beat:** The man asked how anyone would get past the three-headed dog.
- **Image:** The stranger’s pale hand taps the table on the question. Hagrid’s face, in the memory, lights up the way it does when he can talk about a creature he loves. He is proud of Fluffy. That pride is the trap.
- **Setting:** The pub table, close, memory.
- **Who:** The stranger’s hands, hood edge. Hagrid leaning in, fond of his dog.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks proud, a little drunk, about to boast. The hood tilts, hungry for the answer. No smile we can trust.
- **Dialogue:** **Stranger:** "A three-headed dog on a trapdoor. However would a wizard get past a beast like that?"

#### C16-P2-P3

- **Beat:** Hagrid swears he never said, then admits he said music puts Fluffy to sleep.
- **Image:** Back in the hut, present. Hagrid’s hands are up in denial, then they fall, and he admits it into the handkerchief. Hermione’s eyes close. The word music sits in the room like a key.
- **Setting:** The hut, afternoon, tea gone cold.
- **Who:** Hagrid, vest, miserable honesty. Hermione, eyes shut, one hand on her brow. Harry very straight. Ron staring.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks at the table, ashamed. Hermione looks at nothing, the plan of the thief completing itself behind her eyelids. Harry looks at Hagrid, not angry, afraid. Ron looks at the door, as if the thief might be on the step.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "I never said a useful thing. Well. I said music. Play a tune and Fluffy goes straight off to sleep."

#### C16-P2-P4

- **Beat:** Hermione closes her eyes.
- **Image:** A quiet panel. Just Hermione’s face, eyes closed, summer light, hair. She is not resting. She is seeing the trapdoor and a flute and a stranger who already knows.
- **Setting:** The hut, soft background, Hagrid a blur of beard.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes, a line between her brows, hands clasped on the table.
- **Faces:** Eyes shut. Mouth tight. Fear managed by thinking. When she opens them — we see the lids about to — she will be ready to move.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Then the stranger already has the first key."

#### C16-P2-P5

- **Beat:** Hagrid cannot remember the face. A hood, or maybe a turban.
- **Image:** Hagrid squints into his memory, one hand waving at his own head, hood, turban, he cannot land it. The children watch his hand. The ambiguity is a door Harry will shut too fast.
- **Setting:** The hut. Late light. Fang asleep, the only one at peace.
- **Who:** Hagrid, gesturing around his head, beard, frustrated with himself. Harry, Ron, Hermione watching the gesture as if the face might appear in the air.
- **Faces:** Hagrid looks into the middle distance, trying, failing, guilty. Hermione looks at his hand, filing both possibilities. Ron looks confused. Harry looks certain already, the turban mention sliding off him. He wants the memory to be Snape.
- **Dialogue:** **Hagrid:** "Hood up. Or maybe a turban. I can't hold the face in my head. He kept the drinks coming."

#### C16-P2-P6

- **Beat:** Harry hears “Snape” in his head. The panel shows Quirrell’s turban, and Harry does not see it.
- **Image:** Split. On Harry’s side, his imagination: Snape in the pub hood, black hair, hook nose, sneering over a mug. On the truth side of the panel, unseen by Harry: Quirrell’s purple turban and nervous hands pushing the drink, the hood down a little. A line between them. Harry is looking at the wrong half.
- **Setting:** The hut’s air, a vision panel, summer dust.
- **Who:** Imagined Snape, black, cruel, false. True Quirrell, turban, pale, sweating, the buyer of drinks. Harry in the foreground, glasses, looking at the Snape he invented.
- **Faces:** Imagined Snape looks villainous, which is how Harry needs him. Quirrell looks frightened and purposeful, a worse combination. Harry looks convinced. His certainty is the danger. Hermione, small at the edge, looks at Harry, not sharing the certainty.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Snape. It was Snape in that pub. It has to be."

### Page 3 — The warning refused

#### C16-P3-P1

- **Beat:** They run to McGonagall’s office.
- **Image:** Three children run a sunlit corridor, robes flying, end-of-term clutter, other students with trunks staring. Hermione is fastest. Harry’s scarf trails. They look like trouble and they are trying to prevent it.
- **Setting:** Castle corridor, late afternoon, summer, portraits turning. McGonagall’s office door ahead, stern wood.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair streaming, a plan. Harry, glasses, scar, determined. Ron, red hair, freckles, keeping up, the healed hand swinging.
- **Faces:** They look at the door, urgent. Hermione looks resolved. Harry looks angry at the time they have lost. Ron looks like he hopes she is in.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Professor McGonagall. Please. It's urgent."

#### C16-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Hermione, unusually messy, says someone is going to steal the Stone.
- **Image:** Inside the office. Hermione stands in front of the desk, hair escaped, the speech tumbling, hands moving. She is never this untidy. That is how McGonagall knows it is serious, and also why the scene feels off-balance.
- **Setting:** McGonagall’s office. Green tartan, a desk, neatly stacked exams, a sunbeam, a sleeping cat that might be a teacup. Order, and three children breaking it.
- **Who:** Hermione, robes askew, hands open, pleading the case. McGonagall behind the desk, emerald, spectacles, exams in hand. Harry and Ron flanking, backup.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at McGonagall, urgent, a child asking an adult to be the adult. McGonagall looks at her, listening, already bracing to refuse. Harry looks at McGonagall, willing her to hear. Ron looks at a suit of armor in the corner, nervous in offices.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Someone means to steal the Philosopher's Stone. Hagrid told a stranger in a pub how to get past Fluffy. Music puts the dog to sleep."

#### C16-P3-P3

- **Beat:** McGonagall says it is well protected and they are to stop meddling.
- **Image:** She sets the exams down, aligned, a gesture of the subject being closed. Her hand is up, not unkind, final. The Stone is not their business. The desk between them is a wall.
- **Setting:** The office. Sun. The cat-teacup flicks an ear.
- **Who:** McGonagall, precise, emerald, the raised hand. The three children on the other side, the speech stopped.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at them, firm, a teacher protecting children from a fight she thinks is already guarded. Hermione looks at the hand, the plan hitting an adult no. Harry looks at her, frustration rising. Ron looks at Harry, he knew this would happen.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "The Stone is guarded by more than a dog, Miss Granger. And you three will stop meddling in matters far above your year."

#### C16-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Harry says Snape. Her face closes.
- **Image:** Harry steps beside Hermione and says the name. McGonagall’s warmth shuts. Accusing a colleague crosses a line. Her mouth becomes a line. The sunbeam does not help.
- **Setting:** The office, tighter.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, the accusation. McGonagall, spectacles flashing, emerald, still. Hermione wishing he had kept the name back. Ron wincing.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at her, earnest, he thinks the name is proof. McGonagall looks at him, offended on Snape’s behalf and worried about Harry’s certainty. Hermione looks at Harry, a small shake of the head, too late. The door of the conversation closes in McGonagall’s eyes.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's Professor Snape. He's after the Stone for Voldemort." **McGonagall:** "How dare you, Potter."

#### C16-P3-P5

- **Beat:** She says Dumbledore will be told, and sends them out.
- **Image:** She is around the desk, guiding them by presence, not shoves, toward the door. A promise: Dumbledore will hear. It sounds like safety. It is about to be hollow.
- **Setting:** The office door, corridor bright beyond.
- **Who:** McGonagall, a hand indicating the door, stern care. Hermione looking back over her shoulder. Harry, unsatisfied. Ron, relieved and not.
- **Faces:** McGonagall looks at Harry, she means the promise. Harry looks back, he does not feel safer. Hermione looks at the corridor, thinking ahead. Ron looks at his shoes.
- **Dialogue:** **McGonagall:** "I shall inform Professor Dumbledore myself. Out. All three of you."

#### C16-P3-P6

- **Beat:** A portrait tells them Dumbledore has already left the school.
- **Image:** In the corridor, a portrait of an old witch leans out of her frame, delighted to gossip. Dumbledore was called away. Urgent. A carriage. Gone. The children’s faces fall in a row. The promise is empty.
- **Setting:** The corridor outside the office. Afternoon going gold. The portrait’s painted room behind her, fussy and calm, unlike the news.
- **Who:** The portrait witch, lace cap, eager. Hermione, Harry, Ron, stopped in the sun.
- **Faces:** The portrait looks thrilled with her news. Hermione looks at her, the floor dropping. Harry looks down the corridor, as if he could see the carriage already gone. Ron looks at Harry, the mad plan arriving in his eyes whether he wants it or not. Fear, all three, and no adult left in the building who will hear them.
- **Dialogue:** **Portrait:** "Dumbledore? Oh, he left this afternoon. An urgent owl. The headmaster is not in the castle."

### Page 4 — The choice

#### C16-P4-P1

- **Beat:** The common room. Ron whispers that this is mad.
- **Image:** They have a corner to themselves. Trunks are half-packed around the room, end of term, everyone else happy. Ron leans in, whispering, both hands open: this is mad. He is not refusing. He is naming it.
- **Setting:** Gryffindor common room, evening, gold light, packed trunks, laughter that is not theirs. A last night feeling.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater, freckles, whisper. Harry, scarf off, glasses, listening. Hermione, a book closed, she is past books.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at them, scared, honest, still here. Harry looks at him, grateful he said it. Hermione looks at the happy students, then at Ron, she agrees it is mad. None of them look ready to go back to packing.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "This is mad. We're first years. There's a three-headed dog and a dark wizard."

#### C16-P4-P2

- **Beat:** The other protections fail if the thief already has the first one.
- **Image:** Hermione’s finger taps the table in a short rhythm, like counting locks. One tap is broken: music. The rest are a guess. Her face is the logic, clean and frightened.
- **Setting:** The corner. Evening. A trunk gaping nearby, clothes, a reminder of the train home they might still catch if they stop.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair, robes, tapping finger. Harry and Ron watching the finger as if it were a lockpick.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at her own hand, certain of the logic, hating it. Harry looks at her, the decision settling. Ron looks at the packed trunk, then back, he is not going to the train.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "If the thief knows about the music, the other protections are only a corridor. Fluffy is the front door, and the door is open."

#### C16-P4-P3

- **Beat:** If Voldemort returns, it will be because they did nothing.
- **Image:** Harry says it quietly, no speechifying. His hand is on the scar. The common-room noise continues behind him, obscene in its ordinariness. The sentence makes Ron go still and Hermione close her book that was already closed.
- **Setting:** The corner. Fire. Other children’s laughter, soft.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar under his fingers, the forest still on him. Ron and Hermione, the audience that matters.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at them, not dramatic, just clear. Young and responsible in a way he should not have to be. Hermione looks at him, the last objection folding. Ron looks at him, a swallow, then a nod.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "If Voldemort comes back, it will be because we sat in Gryffindor Tower and did nothing."

#### C16-P4-P4

- **Beat:** They look at each other. The argument ends.
- **Image:** Three faces, close, no more talking. A nod each. The portrait hole is behind them. End of term trunks. They are going tonight.
- **Setting:** The common room, the fire, the decision.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione. No wands out yet. Just the look.
- **Faces:** They look at one another, fear shared so it is lighter. Harry looks steady. Hermione looks brave and tidy again, a plan forming. Ron looks terrified and loyal, a crooked almost-smile. Friends.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "All right. Tonight." **Hermione:** "Tonight."

#### C16-P4-P5

- **Beat:** Night. Three small figures. Harry raises a flute Hagrid gave him.
- **Image:** The third-floor corridor, the one from the feast warning. Moon. They stand before the door. Harry has a small wooden flute, a Hagrid gift from some earlier kindness, raised to his mouth. Hermione has her wand. Ron has his. They look tiny against the door.
- **Setting:** The forbidden corridor, night, high windows, moonlight, the door to Fluffy. Summer night, warm stone, cold errand.
- **Who:** Harry, school robes over clothes, glasses, scar, the flute at his lips, not playing yet. Hermione, wand out, hair tied back for work. Ron, wand, freckles, red hair, a look at the flute like he hopes Hagrid was right. No cloak of invisibility. They are just three children.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the door, the flute a strange gentle weapon. Hermione looks at the lock, ready. Ron looks at Harry, trust. All of them look afraid and finished with waiting.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Hagrid gave me this flute. If music works on Fluffy, it will have to do."

#### C16-P4-P6

- **Beat:** A growl answers from behind the door.
- **Image:** Page-turn. The flute is just about to sound. From behind the door, a growl, low, triple, the door shivering. Dust sifts down. Their eyes go wide. The year of whispers has a throat.
- **Setting:** The door, close. Moonlight. The corridor empty of adults. The growl visible as a tremor in the wood.
- **Who:** The three, small, Harry’s fingers on the flute, Hermione’s wand light starting, Ron’s shoulders up. The door. Fluffy unseen, announced.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the door, a breath in, he will play. Hermione looks at the hinges, calculating. Ron looks at Harry, a silent now. The door looks back, trembling. They go in on the turn of the page.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "He's awake." **Hermione:** "Then play, Harry. Play now."
---

## Chapter 18 — Through the Trapdoor

A sleeping dog, a strangling plant, a flock of keys, a chess sacrifice, and a logic problem with only one swallow forward.

### Page 1 — Fluffy and the snare

#### C17-P1-P1

- **Beat:** Harry plays, badly. The heads droop one at a time.
- **Image:** The door is open. Fluffy fills the room again, three heads snarling — then the flute’s thin tune wobbles out and the left head blinks, heavy. The middle head resists. The right head yawns, enormous teeth and a pink tongue. Harry’s fingers stumble and he keeps going.
- **Setting:** The third-floor room, night, moonlight from a high slit, stone, the trapdoor under the paws. The flute is a small sweet sound in a huge smell of dog.
- **Who:** Fluffy, three heads, shaggy, collars, drool, the left eye already shut. Harry in front, robes, glasses, flute at his mouth, cheeks puffed, scar. Hermione and Ron pressed to the door behind him, wands lit, tiny.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the heads, concentrated, not a musician, a boy who will not stop. The middle head looks at him, suspicious, then glassy. Hermione looks at the paws, waiting for the moment. Ron looks at the teeth, pale, staying.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "It's meant to be a lullaby. Please work." **Hermione:** "One head is down. Don't stop."

#### C17-P1-P2

- **Beat:** They haul a head aside and drop through the trapdoor.
- **Image:** One huge head is a snoring weight they roll by the ear. The trapdoor ring is free. Hermione lifts it. A dark square. They look at each other and drop, Harry last, the flute tucked in his belt. A snore follows them down.
- **Setting:** The room above, the square of darkness below. Moonlight on the dog’s closed eyes. No ladder. A fall.
- **Who:** Hermione lifting the ring, robes, hair tied, determined. Ron sitting on the edge, freckles, red hair, swinging his legs in, scared and going. Harry, flute in his belt, glasses, one look back at Fluffy to be sure. The dog, asleep, three tongues out, ridiculous and lethal.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks down, a swallow, then she goes. Ron looks at Harry, a crooked brave grin. Harry looks at the sleeping heads, a flash of thanks to Hagrid’s music, then he jumps. Fluffy does not look at anything.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "No ladder. Brilliant." **Harry:** "Jump."

#### C17-P1-P3

- **Beat:** They land in something soft. It feels lucky.
- **Image:** They hit a mass of pale vines and sink, laughing once with relief. It is soft. It is dark. Harry’s glasses are askew and he is grinning. For one panel the fall was the danger and the landing is kind.
- **Setting:** A pit under the trapdoor. Wand-light. Thick pale plants, leaves like fingers, a dirt smell, no floor visible. The trapdoor a small square of moonlight far above.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione waist-deep in vines, robes rumpled, wands up, the flute still in Harry’s belt. They look like children who got away with something.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at Ron, a breathless laugh. Ron looks at the leaves, pleased. Hermione looks at the vines, the laugh dying first, she recognizes them. Her face changes before theirs.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Soft landing. We are the luckiest idiots in Hogwarts."

#### C17-P1-P4

- **Beat:** Vines tighten. Ron is pulled under.
- **Image:** The plants wake. Loops cinch. Ron yelps and is yanked down to his chest, then his shoulders. A vine crosses his mouth. Harry grabs his wrist and is pulled too. The softness was a mouth.
- **Setting:** The pit, wand-light swinging, leaves wrapping, moonlight far above useless.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, eyes huge, one arm up, the rest disappearing into pale green. Harry, both hands on that wrist, glasses, robes caught. Hermione higher on a thicker stem, one arm free, wand in it. Devil’s Snare, many fingers, no face, patient.
- **Faces:** Ron looks up at Harry, panic, he cannot shout. Harry looks at Ron’s disappearing shoulder, fear, pulling. Hermione looks at the plant, thinking hard, afraid and angry at it. The plant has no face. Its grip is the expression.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "It's got me! Harry, it's pulling me under!"

#### C17-P1-P5

- **Beat:** Hermione shouts that Devil’s Snare hates light.
- **Image:** She has it. Her mouth is open on the name. Her wand is already turning. Harry looks at her, lost, still pulling. Ron’s fingers slip in his. The knowledge is a light before the spell is.
- **Setting:** The pit, dark leaves, her face the brightest thing.
- **Who:** Hermione, hair escaping the tie, robes, wand up, the answer in her whole posture. Harry straining. Ron’s hand, freckled, weakening.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the vines, fierce, certain. She looks at the boys, a command in her eyes: do not fight it, I am here. Harry looks at her, trusting. Ron’s eyes look at her, the panic pausing half a second.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Devil's Snare! Devil's Snare hates light and warmth. Stop struggling, Ron!"

#### C17-P1-P6

- **Beat:** She conjures fire. The vines recoil. They crawl free, coughing.
- **Image:** A bright, controlled fire bursts from her wand, blue-white then honest flame. The vines whip back like burned fingers. Ron surges up, gasping. They scramble onto a stone lip. Smoke. The plant sulks in the dark behind them.
- **Setting:** The edge of the pit, a passage beginning, stone, their wands and the fire the only light. The trapdoor moon above, left behind.
- **Who:** Hermione, wand burning, hair wild, soot on her cheek, robes. Ron on hands and knees, coughing, red hair full of leaves, freckles, alive. Harry pulling him, glasses smudged, flute safe, a vine-burn on his wrist.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the retreating plant, shaken and proud. Ron looks at her, coughing, adoring gratitude. Harry looks at Ron, making sure, then at Hermione, a nod that is a thank-you. They look singed and together.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Incendio!" **Ron:** "I hate that plant. I hate it politely."

### Page 2 — The keys

#### C17-P2-P1

- **Beat:** A room of wings. Hundreds of keys batter the air.
- **Image:** The next chamber is tall and bright with motion. Hundreds of winged keys whirl, gold and silver and brass, a storm with teeth. The door on the far side is locked. A broom leans by the near door, waiting for a Seeker.
- **Setting:** A stone room, high windows or none, torch brackets, the air itself occupied. The locked door, old and heavy, a keyhole.
- **Who:** The keys, small metal bodies, delicate wings, hostile. Harry, Ron, Hermione in the doorway, leaves still in their hair, looking up. The broom, old, school-issued, against the wall.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the broom, recognition, the courtyard and the pitch in his hands already. Ron looks at the flock, ducking a key. Hermione looks at the lock, then at Harry, she knows this one is his. A key looks, somehow, vicious.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Winged keys. The door is locked. One of them fits the keyhole."

#### C17-P2-P2

- **Beat:** Harry takes the broom without being asked.
- **Image:** He is astride before the plan is spoken. Hermione opens her mouth and closes it. Ron hands him nothing; there is nothing to hand. The flock rustles, interested.
- **Setting:** The near wall. The broom. The storm of keys above.
- **Who:** Harry, robes, glasses, scar, leaves in his hair, hands right on the broom, the flute no longer the point. Hermione, a half-gesture of be careful. Ron, a thumbs-up he immediately regrets as uncool and leaves up anyway.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the lock, then at the flock, calm the way he is only in the air. Hermione looks at him, trust. Ron looks at him, pride. Harry does not look back for long. The job is up.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "That's a broom. This room was built for a Seeker."

#### C17-P2-P3

- **Beat:** He flies. The keys swirl like a hostile flock.
- **Image:** He kicks off and the room becomes sky. Keys dive at his face. He banks. A silver one clips his ear. The flock turns as a school of fish. He is the only living shape among metal wings.
- **Setting:** The full height of the room. Torches. The locked door small below. Ron and Hermione tiny, shouting clues he half hears.
- **Who:** Harry, scarlet-less school robes streaming anyway, glasses, broom, a line of blood starting at his ear or hand. The keys, a whirlwind. His friends, two upturned faces.
- **Faces:** Harry looks through the flock, searching, not swatting, Seeker-eyes. Pain flickers and gets filed. Hermione looks up, tracking a large old key with her finger. Ron looks up, flinching for him.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Left! No, your other left!"

#### C17-P2-P4

- **Beat:** One old silver key, bent wing.
- **Image:** There. Among the bright new keys, one heavy old silver key with a bent wing, favoring one side, circling near the door it belongs to. Hermione’s pointing finger and Harry’s gaze meet on it. It knows it has been seen and darts.
- **Setting:** Mid-air, the flock parting for one second around the old key. Torchlight on silver.
- **Who:** The old key, tarnished, one wing crooked, larger than the others. Harry banking hard toward it. Hermione below, arm straight, she found it. Ron hopping.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at the bent wing, focus narrowing to a point. The key, if metal can panic, panics. Hermione looks at Harry, a fierce go. Her face is a teammate’s.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "The old one! Silver, with the bent wing. That's the key!"

#### C17-P2-P5

- **Beat:** It fights and cuts his hand. His fist closes on it.
- **Image:** The catch is not clean. The key rakes his palm, a bright cut, wings beating against his knuckles. He closes his fist anyway. Blood and silver. He pulls up before the wall.
- **Setting:** Close to the far wall, the locked door under him, torches.
- **Who:** Harry’s hand, the key crushed carefully inside it, wings still struggling, blood between his fingers. His face, glasses, a grimace that is also a grin. The broom.
- **Faces:** He looks at his fist, pained, certain he has the right one. The key does not stop fighting. He looks like the boy who caught the Remembrall, older by a year of Saturdays.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Got you. Stop cutting me."

#### C17-P2-P6

- **Beat:** The lock turns. They run through before the flock regroups.
- **Image:** He is down, the key in the lock, the door swinging. The flock, cheated, dives as one. Hermione yanks Ron through. Harry kicks the door shut. A dozen keys ping off the wood. Silence, then their breath.
- **Setting:** The doorway between rooms. Behind, the angry metallic storm. Ahead, a cold quiet and a chessboard glimpsed.
- **Who:** Harry, bleeding hand, broom left behind, glasses, shoulder to the shut door. Hermione pulling Ron. Ron, red hair, a key-cut on his cheek, laughing once in shock. The old key, spent, in the lock.
- **Faces:** Harry looks at his hand, then at them, a shaky smile. Hermione looks at the cut, already thinking of the next room, a thank-you in the look. Ron looks at the door, giddy to be unpecked. They run forward.
- **Dialogue:** **Harry:** "Through, before the flock comes back."

### Page 3 — The chessboard

#### C17-P3-P1

- **Beat:** Giant chess. The pieces are stone and armed.
- **Image:** A hall-sized board. The squares are the floor. The pieces are taller than the children, carved stone, swords and halberds real enough. They turn their heads as the three enter. A king. A queen with a blank, terrible face. The far door is beyond the last rank.
- **Setting:** A cold chamber, black and white squares, torchlight, no windows. Echo. The pieces’ shadows are longer than they are.
- **Who:** Harry, Ron, Hermione, small at the edge, leaves and cuts and wands. The white pieces nearest, a pawn’s stone eyes rolling down. The black pieces waiting on the far side, hostile.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the board, and for the first time tonight he looks at home. The fear is still there. The understanding is bigger. Harry looks at Ron, seeing it. Hermione looks at the queen, wary. A pawn looks at them, impassive, waiting to be commanded or to crush them.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Wizard's chess. Life size. Don't step on a square until I say so."

#### C17-P3-P2

- **Beat:** Ron walks the board the way other people read. He places his friends.
- **Image:** Ron steps onto the squares without flinching. He points Harry to a bishop’s empty place, Hermione to a rook’s. He talks with his hands, the fever and the jokes gone, a commander. The pieces allow it. One knight steps aside for him.
- **Setting:** The board, mid-field, torches, the huge pieces making aisles.
- **Who:** Ron, maroon sweater under robes, freckles, red hair, bandaged no longer, the bitten hand gesturing, sure. Harry moving to a bishop square, trusting. Hermione to a castle square, a book-person suddenly a piece, accepting it. A stone knight, horse-head, stepping back.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the whole board, not at his fear. Calm, bright, the best of him. Harry looks at Ron, respect, he will go where he is told. Hermione looks at Ron, a new understanding, she is not the clever one in this room. The knight looks at Ron, as if recognizing a player.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Harry, you're a bishop. Diagonals only. Hermione, you're the castle, the rook, straight lines. I'll take the knight."

#### C17-P3-P3

- **Beat:** Harry a bishop. Hermione a castle. Ron takes a knight.
- **Image:** They are in place. Harry stands on a bishop’s square, diagonal world. Hermione on a rook’s, straight lines. Ron swings onto the stone knight’s back, small on the carved horse. The black pieces stir. The game can start.
- **Setting:** The board from slightly above, so the squares read. Three children among giants.
- **Who:** Harry, glasses, scar, bleeding hand, on his square, wand in his sleeve. Hermione, hair, robes, serious. Ron astride the knight, red hair a flag, freckles, a nod to begin. Stone pieces, armed.
- **Faces:** Ron looks across at the black king, already ten moves ahead. Harry looks at Ron, waiting for the order. Hermione looks along her line, ready to move when told. She looks like she hates not knowing the plan and trusts it anyway.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "White plays. Do exactly as I call it. These pieces do not take prisoners."

#### C17-P3-P4

- **Beat:** Pieces smash each other apart. Dust and swords.
- **Image:** The game is violent. A black pawn explodes into rubble under a white piece’s sword. Dust rolls. Hermione’s rook charges and she hangs on, hair whipping, a shout of effort. A stone head rolls past Harry’s foot. This is not a classroom board.
- **Setting:** The board in chaos. Torchlight through dust. Cracks in the floor. The far door still shut.
- **Who:** Hermione riding or running her piece, fierce, a scrape on her cheek. Harry moved diagonal, a bishop’s lunge, glasses dusty. Ron on the knight, shouting a move, in his element and terrified. Stone soldiers breaking.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at the wreckage, calculating, a wince when a piece dies, he feels it. Hermione looks at the next square, determined, not screaming. Harry looks at Ron for the next order, a soldier. The black queen turns her blank face toward Ron’s knight.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Hermione, two squares straight. Harry, diagonal, now. Don't you flinch."

#### C17-P3-P5

- **Beat:** Ron sees the only win and rides forward to be taken.
- **Image:** Quiet inside the noise. Ron sees it. His face goes still. He looks at Harry, then at the queen, then he smiles a small, crooked, serious smile and puts his heels to the knight. He rides into the square where the queen can take him. He does not look like a boy messing about. He looks like a choice.
- **Setting:** The board, dust settling for one second. The queen’s shadow over his square. The far door close now.
- **Who:** Ron, red hair, freckles, maroon sleeves, on the knight, small. The black queen, tall, sword, blank face, turning toward him. Harry several squares away, realizing too late. Hermione further, a cry starting.
- **Faces:** Ron looks at Harry, the smile, a go-on already in it. Love and terror and pride. Harry looks at him, no, the word not out yet. The queen looks at Ron, nothing human, the sword rising. Hermione looks at Ron, understanding a second after Harry, horror.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "There's only one win. The queen has to take me. Harry, when she does, you move on the king. Checkmate." **Harry:** "Ron, no." **Ron:** "That's an order, bishop."

#### C17-P3-P6

- **Beat:** The queen strikes him down. He has already told them to go on.
- **Image:** The sword comes down. Ron is flung from the knight, a small body on the stone, still. Dust. The king is in check from the move he bought; Harry’s square is the mate, and he will have to take it. Ron’s eyes are closed. His hand is open toward the far door.
- **Setting:** The board. Rubble. Torch. The door beyond the king.
- **Who:** Ron on the stones, red hair fanned, freckles, a bruise already, breathing — he is alive and out. The queen, toppled or spent by the sequence, not the focus. Harry on his bishop square, the winning move under his feet, looking back. Hermione running toward Ron and stopping because the game is not over until they move.
- **Faces:** Ron’s face is slack, a ghost of the smile, unconscious. Harry looks at him, wrecked, then at the door, because Ron already told him. Hermione looks at Ron, tears she blinks away, then at Harry, we have to finish. Grief and obedience.
- **Dialogue:** **Ron:** "Go on. Both of you. Checkmate, then the door. I'm all right. Go."

### Page 4 — Logic and goodbye

#### C17-P4-P1

- **Beat:** A troll, already unconscious. Hermione does not slow down.
- **Image:** The next room has a mountain troll, the bathroom’s cousin, flat on its back, snoring, club loose. It is a solved problem. Hermione steps around its hand without looking twice. Harry follows, the chess-dust still on him. They do not have time to be surprised.
- **Setting:** A stinking stone room, the troll, a door beyond. Torchlight. No fight.
- **Who:** The troll, gray, huge, asleep, harmless as a hill. Hermione, hair, a cut on her cheek, robes, walking. Harry, bleeding hand, glasses, looking back once toward where Ron lies in the previous room, then forward.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the next door, focused, she will not spend Ron’s move on a fainted troll. Harry looks at the troll, a grim almost-laugh, then at her back, following. The troll’s face is slack, tongue out, comic and ignored.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "The troll is already down. We don't stop for a solved room."

#### C17-P4-P2

- **Beat:** Seven bottles and a riddle.
- **Image:** A table. Seven bottles of different sizes, stoppers, liquids: some clear, some amber, one ruby, one black. A riddle burned into a parchment or into the air in thin flame. Two doors: one back, one forward. No monster. A brain.
- **Setting:** A small chamber, cleaner, colder, the table centered. Torches. The forward door iron, the back door the way they came.
- **Who:** Hermione and Harry, alone, dusty, cut, standing before the bottles. No Ron. The riddle is the third presence.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at the riddle, the world narrowing to logic, lips parting. Harry looks at the bottles, then at her, he knows this is hers the way the keys were his. He looks relieved and afraid of that relief.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Seven bottles, and a logic riddle. This is Professor Snape's idea of a welcome."

#### C17-P4-P3

- **Beat:** She reads it once, then again, lips moving.
- **Image:** Close. Her eyes move. Her lips shape the puzzle. A finger lifts, counting bottles without touching them. Harry does not interrupt. The chess bruise of the evening is on both of them and she sets it aside to think.
- **Setting:** The table, bottle-light, her face.
- **Who:** Hermione, bushy hair singed at one tip, a scratch, robes, empty hands, the respect of not touching until she knows. Harry a soft presence at the edge, watching her work.
- **Faces:** She looks at the riddle, utterly concentrated, the eager classroom girl become something quieter and stronger. A flicker of fear she will be slow. Harry looks at her, trust, he does not hover. His face says take the time. Ron bought it.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "Poison, wine, and two that are safe. Don't touch a cork until I say."

#### C17-P4-P4

- **Beat:** One swallow goes back. One swallow goes forward.
- **Image:** Her hand finally moves. She touches two bottles. One small, for the way back, enough for one. One smaller, for the way on, enough for one. The other five are death or wine or nettle, and she leaves them. The puzzle is solved in the placement of her fingers.
- **Setting:** The table, top-down feeling, seven bottles, two chosen.
- **Who:** Hermione’s hands, ink from another life under the dust, sure. Harry’s hand not touching any bottle. The two chosen vials, one clear and cold-looking, one tiny and bright.
- **Faces:** Only her mouth and eyes at the top of the frame, certain, sad, because the amounts mean a goodbye. Harry’s eyes at the side, he has understood. They look at the two bottles, not at each other yet.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "This small bottle goes forward. This one goes back. A swallow in each. The other five will kill you."

#### C17-P4-P5

- **Beat:** She says she is not the one who should face what is next, and puts the forward bottle in Harry’s hand.
- **Image:** She presses the tiny bottle into his bleeding hand, careful of the cut. She keeps the other. The iron door is behind him. Her face is close, urgent, not a speech, a decision. She is sending him and staying with the way back to Ron.
- **Setting:** The chamber. The forward door. Torchlight. Two children at the end of the trio.
- **Who:** Hermione, the back bottle in her other hand, hair, scratch, robes. Harry, glasses, scar, the tiny bottle in the cut palm, flute still absurd in his belt, chess dust. No one else.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at Harry, fierce love, fear, she hates this part. She looks at his cut hand, practical even now. Harry looks at her, he wants to argue and does not. Gratitude. A goodbye neither calls a goodbye. His jaw is set.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "I am not the one who should walk through that door. You are. I'll take the way back to Ron." **Harry:** "Hermione." **Hermione:** "Drink. And don't you dare waste a drop."

#### C17-P4-P6

- **Beat:** He drinks. The door opens. Her face is the last thing he sees as it shuts.
- **Image:** Page-turn. He drinks the swallow. Cold fire. The iron door opens on black. He steps through. It begins to close. Through the narrowing gap, Hermione’s face, one hand raised, not quite a wave, the other bottle clutched. Then the door shuts. He is alone.
- **Setting:** The threshold. Behind her, bottles and torch. Ahead of him, darkness, a hint of a tall frame that might be a mirror. The door, iron, closing.
- **Who:** Harry, alone on the far side, the taste of the potion, glasses, scar, empty tiny bottle dropped. Hermione on the near side, reaching, then gone as the door meets stone.
- **Faces:** Hermione looks at him, brave, eyes bright, stay alive. Harry looks at her, the same, a small nod. When the door shuts, his face is alone in the dark, the nod unfinished. The year of three becomes one.
- **Dialogue:** **Hermione:** "We will be behind you as soon as Ron can stand." **Harry:** "I know."
